import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Stage, Layer, Rect, Transformer } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { Slide, AnchorSide, CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore, useModalsStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { ElementRenderer } from './ElementRenderer';
import { GridLayer } from './GridLayer';
import { CanvasPreviewLayer } from './canvas/CanvasPreviewLayer';
import { findElementAt } from '../../domain/slide/hitTest';
import { useCanvasViewport } from '../hooks/canvas/useCanvasViewport';
import {
  useCanvasDrag, useCanvasRubberBand, useCanvasFillMode,
  useCanvasConnectorDraft, useCanvasDrop, useCanvasHoverAnchor,
  useCanvasDblClick,
} from '../hooks/canvas';

interface Props {
  slide: Slide;
  zoom: number;
  onZoomChange: (z: number) => void;
}

interface GroupDragState {
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
  orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>;
}

export const Canvas: React.FC<Props> = ({ slide, zoom, onZoomChange }) => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();

  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);

  const isDraggingRef = useRef(false);
  const groupDragRef = useRef<GroupDragState | null>(null);

  const elementsRef = useRef(slide.elements);
  if (elementsRef.current !== slide.elements) elementsRef.current = slide.elements;

  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);
  const editing = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.editing);
  const fillMode = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.fillMode);

  const viewport = useCanvasViewport({ zoom, onZoomChange, stageRef });
  const drag = useCanvasDrag({ docStore, selStore, modalsStore, slide });
  const rubber = useCanvasRubberBand({ selStore });
  const fill = useCanvasFillMode({ docStore, modalsStore, slide });
  const draft = useCanvasConnectorDraft({ docStore, slide });
  const drop = useCanvasDrop({ docStore, stageRef, zoom });
  const hover = useCanvasHoverAnchor({ slide });
  const dbl = useCanvasDblClick({ modalsStore, zoom });

  /* Sync Transformer */
  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!tr || !stage) return;
    if (!selectedIds.length) { tr.nodes([]); tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw(); return; }
    const nodes = selectedIds.map((id) => {
      for (const l of stage.getLayers()) {
        const n = l.findOne('#' + id);
        if (n) return n;
      }
      return null;
    }).filter((n): n is Konva.Node => !!n);
    tr.nodes(nodes);
    tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedIds, slide.elements]);

  /* Dblclick */
  useEffect(() => {
    const onDblClick = (evt: MouseEvent) => {
      if (viewport.isPanning) return;
      const stage = stageRef.current;
      if (!stage) return;
      const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
      if (!pointer) return;

      const wx = pointer.x / zoom;
      const wy = pointer.y / zoom;

      if (fill.points().length >= 6) { fill.closePolygon(); return; }

      const el = findElementAt(slide.elements, wx, wy);
      if (!el) return;

      const rect = stage.container().getBoundingClientRect();
      dbl.handle(el, { viewportLeft: rect.left, viewportTop: rect.top, clientX: evt.clientX, clientY: evt.clientY });
    };

    window.addEventListener('dblclick', onDblClick);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('dblclick', onDblClick);
  }, [slide.elements, zoom, fill, dbl, viewport.isPanning]);

  const handleStartDraft = useCallback((id: string, side: AnchorSide, clientX: number, clientY: number) => {
    if (viewport.isPanning) return;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
    if (!pointer) return;

    const wx = pointer.x / zoom;
    const wy = pointer.y / zoom;
    draft.startDraft(id, side, wx, wy);
  }, [zoom, draft, viewport.isPanning]);

  /* вЂ”вЂ”вЂ” Stage handlers вЂ”вЂ”вЂ” */

  const onStageDown = useCallback((e: any) => {
    if (viewport.isPanning) { viewport.handleMouseDown(e.evt); return; }
    const stage = e.target.getStage();
    const isStage = e.target === stage;
    const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
    if (!p) return;

    const wx = p.x / zoom;
    const wy = p.y / zoom;

    if (fillMode) {
      if (isStage) fill.applyFill(wx, wy);
      return;
    }

    /* Р“СЂСѓРїРїРѕРІРѕР№ РґСЂР°Рі: РєР»РёРє РЅР° РїСѓСЃС‚РѕРј РјРµСЃС‚Рµ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё bbox РІС‹РґРµР»РµРЅРёСЏ */
    if (isStage && selectedIds.length > 1) {
      const boxes = selectedIds
        .map((id) => slide.elements.find((el) => el.id === id))
        .filter(Boolean) as CanvasElement[];

      if (boxes.length > 0) {
        const minX = Math.min(...boxes.map((b) => b.x));
        const minY = Math.min(...boxes.map((b) => b.y));
        const maxX = Math.max(...boxes.map((b) => b.x + b.width));
        const maxY = Math.max(...boxes.map((b) => b.y + b.height));

        if (wx >= minX && wx <= maxX && wy >= minY && wy <= maxY) {
          const orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
          for (const el of boxes) orig[el.id] = { x: el.x, y: el.y };
          groupDragRef.current = { startX: wx, startY: wy, orig };
          isDraggingRef.current = true;
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    if (isStage) {
      selStore.clear();
      modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);
      rubber.begin(wx, wy);
    }
  }, [zoom, fillMode, fill, selStore, modalsStore, rubber, viewport, selectedIds, slide.elements]);

  const onStageMove = useCallback((e: any) => {
    if (viewport.isPanning) return;

    const stage = e.target.getStage();
    const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
    if (!p) return;
    const wx = p.x / zoom;
    const wy = p.y / zoom;

    /* Р“СЂСѓРїРїРѕРІРѕР№ РґСЂР°Рі */
    if (groupDragRef.current && stage) {
      const g = groupDragRef.current;
      const dx = wx - g.startX;
      const dy = wy - g.startY;
      for (const [id, o] of Object.entries(g.orig)) {
        const node = stage.findOne('#' + id);
        if (node) { node.x(o.x + dx); node.y(o.y + dy); }
      }
      stage.batchDraw();
      return;
    }

    if (isDraggingRef.current) return;

    draft.update(wx, wy);
    rubber.update(wx, wy);
    hover.update(e, wx, wy);
  }, [zoom, draft, rubber, hover, viewport.isPanning]);

  const onStageUp = useCallback((e: any) => {
    if (viewport.isPanning) return;

    /* Р—Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅРёРµ РіСЂСѓРїРїРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РґСЂР°РіР° */
    if (groupDragRef.current) {
      const g = groupDragRef.current;
      const stage = e.target.getStage();
      const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
      if (p) {
        const wx = p.x / zoom;
        const wy = p.y / zoom;
        const dx = wx - g.startX;
        const dy = wy - g.startY;

        if (Math.abs(dx) > 1 || Math.abs(dy) > 1) {
          docStore.commit((draftState) => {
            const s = draftState.slides.find((x) => x.id === draftState.activeId);
            if (!s) return;
            for (const el of s.elements) {
              const o = g.orig[el.id];
              if (!o) continue;
              el.x = o.x + dx;
              el.y = o.y + dy;
            }
          }, 'move-group');
        }
      }
      groupDragRef.current = null;
      isDraggingRef.current = false;
      return;
    }

    if (draft.isDrafting()) {
      const stage = e.target.getStage();
      const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
      if (p) {
        const wx = p.x / zoom;
        const wy = p.y / zoom;
        draft.commit(wx, wy);
      }
    }
    if (rubber.isActive()) {
      rubber.finish(slide.elements);
    }
  }, [zoom, draft, rubber, slide.elements, viewport.isPanning, docStore]);

  return (
    <div className="stage-wrap" data-fill-mode={fillMode || undefined} onDragOver={drop.onDragOver} onDrop={drop.onDrop}>
      <Stage
        ref={stageRef}
        width={slide.width * zoom}
        height={slide.height * zoom}
        scaleX={zoom}
        scaleY={zoom}
        onWheel={viewport.handleWheel}
        onMouseDown={onStageDown}
        onMouseMove={onStageMove}
        onMouseUp={onStageUp}
        onContextMenu={(e) => {
          e.evt.preventDefault();
          modalsStore.setContextMenu({ x: e.evt.clientX, y: e.evt.clientY, elementId: null });
        }}
        style={{ cursor: viewport.isPanning ? 'grab' : 'default' }}
      >
        <Layer listening={false}>
          <Rect x={0} y={0} width={slide.width} height={slide.height} fill={slide.background} />
          <GridLayer width={slide.width} height={slide.height} />
          <Rect x={slide.padding.left} y={slide.padding.top} width={Math.max(0, slide.width - slide.padding.left - slide.padding.right)} height={Math.max(0, slide.height - slide.padding.top - slide.padding.bottom)} stroke="#3b82f6" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
        </Layer>

        <Layer>
          {slide.elements.map((el) => (
            <ElementRenderer
              key={el.id}
              element={el}
              elements={elementsRef.current}
              isSelected={selectedIds.includes(el.id)}
              isEditing={editing?.id === el.id}
              onSelect={() => { if (!viewport.isPanning) selStore.select(el.id); }}
              onUpdate={(patch, newElement) => {
                docStore.commit((d) => {
                  const s = d.slides.find((x) => x.id === d.activeId);
                  const target = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === el.id);
                  if (target) Object.assign(target, patch);
                  if (newElement && s) s.elements.push(newElement);
                }, `upd-${el.id}`);
              }}
              onDragStart={(e) => {
                if (draft.isDrafting() || viewport.isPanning) { e.target.stopDrag(); return; }
                isDraggingRef.current = true;
                drag.onDragStart(el, e);
              }}
              onDragMove={(e) => drag.onDragMove(e)}
              onDragEnd={(e) => {
                drag.onDragEnd(el, e);
                isDraggingRef.current = false;
              }}
              onContextMenu={(ev: any) => {
                ev.evt.preventDefault();
                if (!selectedIds.includes(el.id)) selStore.select(el.id);
                modalsStore.setContextMenu({ x: ev.evt.clientX, y: ev.evt.clientY, elementId: el.id });
              }}
            />
          ))}

          <Transformer
            ref={trRef}
            rotateEnabled
            keepRatio={false}
            rotationSnaps={[0, 15, 30, 45, 60, 75, 90, 105, 120, 135, 150, 165, 180, 195, 210, 225, 240, 255, 270, 285, 300, 315, 330, 345]}
            rotationSnapTolerance={6}
            borderStroke="#f59e0b"
            anchorStroke="#f59e0b"
            anchorFill="#fbbf24"
            anchorSize={10}
            /* Transformer С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ СЂРµСЃР°Р№Р·Р°. РќРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ draggable. */
            shouldOverdrawWholeArea={false}
            draggable={false}
            boundBoxFunc={(oldBox, newBox) => {
              if (newBox.width < 20 || newBox.height < 20) return oldBox;
              return newBox;
            }}
            onTransformEnd={(e) => {
              const node = e.target;
              const id = node.id();
              const sx = node.scaleX();
              const sy = node.scaleY();
              node.scaleX(1);
              node.scaleY(1);
              docStore.commit((d) => {
                const s = d.slides.find((x) => x.id === d.activeId);
                const target = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === id);
                if (!target) return;
                target.x = node.x();
                target.y = node.y();
                target.rotation = node.rotation();
                target.width = Math.max(20, target.width * sx);
                target.height = Math.max(20, target.height * sy);
                if (target.type === 'line') {
                  target.points = target.points.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v * sx : v * sy));
                }
              }, `trans-${id}`);
            }}
          />
        </Layer>

        <CanvasPreviewLayer
          zoom={zoom}
          fillPoints={fill.points()}
          draft={draft.current()}
          hoveredId={hover.getHoveredId()}
          elements={slide.elements}
          rubberBand={rubber.ref()}
          onStartDraft={handleStartDraft}
          ghostRect={draft.getGhostRect()}
          selectedIds={selectedIds}
          slideWidth={slide.width}
          slideHeight={slide.height}
        />
      </Stage>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { Layer, Line, Rect, Circle } from 'react-konva';
import type { CanvasElement, AnchorSide } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { Rect as RectType } from '../../../lib/connectors';
import { getAnchorPoint } from '../../../lib/connectors';
import { guidesStore } from '../../stores/GuidesStore';

interface DraftState {
  fromId: string;
  fromSide: AnchorSide;
  toX: number;
  toY: number;
}

interface RubberBandBox {
  x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number;
}

interface Props {
  zoom: number;
  fillPoints: number[];
  draft: DraftState | null;
  hoveredId: string | null;
  elements: CanvasElement[];
  rubberBand: RubberBandBox | null;
  onStartDraft: (id: string, side: AnchorSide, clientX: number, clientY: number) => void;
  ghostRect: RectType | null;
  selectedIds: string[];
  slideWidth: number;
  slideHeight: number;
}

export const CanvasPreviewLayer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  zoom, fillPoints, draft, hoveredId, elements, rubberBand, onStartDraft, ghostRect, selectedIds, slideWidth, slideHeight
}) => {
  const from = draft ? elements.find((x) => x.id === draft.fromId) : null;
  const fromPoint = from ? getAnchorPoint(from, draft!.fromSide) : null;
  const hoveredEl = hoveredId ? elements.find((x) => x.id === hoveredId) : null;
  const showAnchors = hoveredEl && !draft && !selectedIds.includes(hoveredEl.id);

  // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ Р»РёРЅРёРё РёР· СЃС‚РѕСЂР°
  const guides = useSyncExternalStore(guidesStore.subscribe, guidesStore.getSnapshot);

  return (
    <Layer listening={true}>
      {/* Р›РёРЅРёРё РїСЂРёР»РёРїР°РЅРёСЏ */}
      {guides.v.map((x, i) => (
        <Line key={`gv-${i}`} points={[x, 0, x, slideHeight]} stroke="#ef4444" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
      ))}
      {guides.h.map((y, i) => (
        <Line key={`gh-${i}`} points={[0, y, slideWidth, y]} stroke="#ef4444" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
      ))}

      {fillPoints.length >= 2 && (
        <Line points={fillPoints} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom} dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
      )}
      {fillPoints.length >= 2 && Array.from({ length: fillPoints.length / 2 }).map((_, i) => (
        <Rect key={`fp-${i}`} x={fillPoints[i * 2] - 3 / zoom} y={fillPoints[i * 2 + 1] - 3 / zoom} width={6 / zoom} height={6 / zoom} fill="#6366f1" listening={false} />
      ))}

      {draft && fromPoint && (
        <Line points={[fromPoint.x, fromPoint.y, draft.toX, draft.toY]} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom} dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
      )}

      {ghostRect && (
        <Rect x={ghostRect.x} y={ghostRect.y} width={ghostRect.width} height={ghostRect.height} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={2 / zoom} dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} fill="rgba(99,102,241,0.1)" listening={false} />
      )}

      {showAnchors && (['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left'] as AnchorSide[]).map((side) => {
        const pos = getAnchorPoint(hoveredEl, side);
        return (
          <Circle
            key={side} x={pos.x} y={pos.y} radius={6 / zoom}
            fill="#fbbf24" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom}
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onStartDraft(hoveredEl.id, side, e.evt.clientX, e.evt.clientY); }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => { const stage = e.target.getStage(); if (stage) stage.container().style.cursor = 'crosshair'; }}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => { const stage = e.target.getStage(); if (stage) stage.container().style.cursor = 'default'; }}
          />
        );
      })}

      {rubberBand && (
        <Rect x={rubberBand.x1} y={rubberBand.y1} width={rubberBand.x2 - rubberBand.x1} height={rubberBand.y2 - rubberBand.y1} fill="rgba(99,102,241,0.12)" stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom} dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
      )}
    </Layer>
  );
};

/**
 * Context Menu Component - Presentation Layer
 * Right-click menu for elements
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

interface ContextMenuProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  elementId: string | null;
  selectedElements: CanvasElement[];
  onClose: () => void;
  onCut: () => void;
  onCopy: () => void;
  onPaste: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onBringForward: () => void;
  onSendBackward: () => void;
  onCopyStyle: () => void;
  onApplyStyle: () => void;
  canPaste: boolean;
  hasStyleCopied: boolean;
}

export const ContextMenu: React.FC<ContextMenuProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  elementId,
  selectedElements,
  onClose,
  onCut,
  onCopy,
  onPaste,
  onDuplicate,
  onDelete,
  onBringForward,
  onSendBackward,
  onCopyStyle,
  onApplyStyle,
  canPaste,
  hasStyleCopied,
}) => {
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Close on click outside
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    const handleEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  // Clamp position to viewport
  const clampedX = Math.min(x, window.innerWidth - 230);
  const clampedY = Math.min(y, window.innerHeight - 300);

  const hasSelection = selectedElements.length > 0;
  const isLine =
    selectedElements.length === 1 && selectedElements[0].type === 'line';

  return (
    <div
      ref={menuRef}
      className="context-menu"
      style={{ left: clampedX, top: clampedY }}
    >
      {hasSelection && (
        <>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCut}>
            <span>Cut</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+X</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCopy}>
            <span>Copy</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+C</span>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`context-menu-item ${!canPaste ? 'disabled' : ''}`}
            onClick={canPaste ? onPaste : undefined}
          >
            <span>Paste</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+V</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onDuplicate}>
            <span>Duplicate</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+D</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCopyStyle}>
            <span>Copy Style</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Alt+C</span>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`context-menu-item ${!hasStyleCopied ? 'disabled' : ''}`}
            onClick={hasStyleCopied ? onApplyStyle : undefined}
          >
            <span>Apply Style</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Alt+V</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onBringForward}>
            <span>Bring Forward</span>
            <span className="shortcut">]</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onSendBackward}>
            <span>Send Backward</span>
            <span className="shortcut">[</span>
          </div>
          {isLine && (
            <>
              <div className="context-menu-divider" />
              <div className="context-menu-item">
                <span>Add Point</span>
              </div>
              <div className="context-menu-item">
                <span>Remove Last Point</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
        </>
      )}

      <div className="context-menu-item danger" onClick={onDelete}>
        <span>Delete</span>
        <span className="shortcut">Del</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

ContextMenu.displayName = 'ContextMenu';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { DataRoot, DataField, DataFieldType } from '../../shared/types';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

const FIELD_TYPES: DataFieldType[] = ['string', 'number', 'date', 'boolean', 'image', 'object', 'array'];

export const DataPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const data = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.data);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const commit = (recipe: (draft: DataRoot[]) => void) => docStore.commit((draft) => recipe(draft.data), 'data-edit');

  const addRoot = () => commit((d) => d.push({ id: generateId(), name: 'РћР±СЉРµРєС‚', fields: [] }));

  const updateRoot = (rootId: string, patch: Partial<DataRoot>) => commit((d) => { const r = d.find((x) => x.id === rootId); if (r) Object.assign(r, patch); });

  const removeRoot = (rootId: string) => commit((d) => { const i = d.findIndex((x) => x.id === rootId); if (i >= 0) d.splice(i, 1); });

  const updateField = (rootId: string, fieldId: string, patch: Partial<DataField>) => {
    const walk = (fields: DataField[]) => {
      for (const f of fields) { if (f.id === fieldId) { Object.assign(f, patch); return true; } if (f.children && walk(f.children)) return true; }
      return false;
    };
    commit((d) => { const r = d.find((x) => x.id === rootId); if (r) walk(r.fields); });
  };

  const addField = (rootId: string, parentId?: string) => {
    const newField: DataField = { id: generateId(), name: 'РџРѕР»Рµ', type: 'string', sample: '' };
    commit((d) => {
      const r = d.find((x) => x.id === rootId); if (!r) return;
      if (parentId) {
        const walk = (fields: DataField[]): boolean => {
          for (const f of fields) { if (f.id === parentId) { (f.children ??= []).push(newField); return true; } if (f.children && walk(f.children)) return true; }
          return false;
        };
        walk(r.fields);
      } else r.fields.push(newField);
    });
  };

  const removeField = (rootId: string, fieldId: string) => {
    const walk = (fields: DataField[]): boolean => {
      const i = fields.findIndex((f) => f.id === fieldId);
      if (i >= 0) { fields.splice(i, 1); return true; }
      for (const f of fields) if (f.children && walk(f.children)) return true;
      return false;
    };
    commit((d) => { const r = d.find((x) => x.id === rootId); if (r) walk(r.fields); });
  };

  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, payload: object) => {
    e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', JSON.stringify(payload));
    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy';
  };

  const renderField = (root: DataRoot, field: DataField, path: string, depth = 0): React.ReactNode => {
    const isContainer = field.type === 'object' || field.type === 'array';
    const ownPath = `${path}.${field.name}`;
    const isOpen = expanded[field.id] !== false;

    return (
      <div key={field.id} style={{ paddingLeft: depth * 12, marginBottom: 4 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          {isContainer ? (
            <button style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 10 }} onClick={() => setExpanded((e) => ({ ...e, [field.id]: !isOpen }))}>{isOpen ? 'в–ј' : 'в–¶'}</button>
          ) : <span style={{ width: 16 }} />}
          <input style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 40, padding: '4px 6px', fontSize: 12, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 4 }} value={field.name} onChange={(e) => updateField(root.id, field.id, { name: e.target.value })} />
          <select style={{ width: 80, padding: '4px', fontSize: 11, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 4 }} value={field.type} onChange={(e) => updateField(root.id, field.id, { type: e.target.value as DataFieldType })}>
            {FIELD_TYPES.map((t) => <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>)}
          </select>
          <button style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#dc2626' }} onClick={() => removeField(root.id, field.id)}>Г—</button>
        </div>

        {!isContainer && (
          <div draggable onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, { kind: 'field', path: ownPath, fieldType: field.type, sample: field.sample })} style={{ display: 'inline-block', margin: '4px 0 4px 22px', padding: '3px 8px', background: '#dbeafe', border: '1px solid #93c5fd', borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 11, cursor: 'grab' }}>
            в‡± {field.name} <span style={{ color: '#6b7280', fontSize: 9 }}>{field.type}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {field.type === 'array' && (
          <div draggable onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, { kind: 'array', path: ownPath, arrayName: field.name, sampleCount: field.sampleCount ?? 3, arrayChildren: (field.children ?? []).map((c) => ({ name: c.name, type: c.type })) })} style={{ display: 'inline-block', margin: '4px 0 4px 22px', padding: '3px 8px', background: '#fef3c7', border: '1px solid #fcd34d', borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 11, cursor: 'grab' }}>
            в‡± {ownPath} <span style={{ color: '#92400e', fontSize: 9 }}>в†’ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р°</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {isContainer && isOpen && (
          <div style={{ marginLeft: 4, borderLeft: '2px solid #e2e8f0', paddingLeft: 4 }}>
            {field.children?.map((c) => renderField(root, c, ownPath, depth + 1))}
            <button style={{ margin: '4px 0 4px 22px', padding: '2px 8px', fontSize: 10, border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1', borderRadius: 4, background: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer' }} onClick={() => addField(root.id, field.id)}>+ РїРѕР»Рµ</button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 10, overflowY: 'auto', height: '100%' }}>
      <button style={{ width: '100%', padding: 6, marginBottom: 8, background: '#6366f1', color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 4, cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 12 }} onClick={addRoot}>+ РљРѕСЂРµРЅСЊ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…</button>
      {data.length === 0 && <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8', textAlign: 'center', margin: 20 }}>РќРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…. РЎРѕР·РґР°Р№С‚Рµ РєРѕСЂРµРЅСЊ Рё РїРµСЂРµС‚Р°С‰РёС‚Рµ РїРѕР»Рµ РЅР° С…РѕР»СЃС‚.</p>}
      {data.map((root) => {
        const isOpen = expanded[root.id] !== false;
        return (
          <div key={root.id} style={{ marginBottom: 8, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 6, background: '#fff' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, padding: 6, background: '#eef2ff', borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
              <button style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 10 }} onClick={() => setExpanded((e) => ({ ...e, [root.id]: !isOpen }))}>{isOpen ? 'в–ј' : 'в–¶'}</button>
              <input style={{ flex: 1, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13, padding: '3px 6px', border: '1px solid transparent', borderRadius: 4, background: 'transparent' }} value={root.name} onChange={(e) => updateRoot(root.id, { name: e.target.value })} />
              <button style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#dc2626' }} onClick={() => removeRoot(root.id)}>Г—</button>
            </div>
            {isOpen && (
              <div style={{ padding: 8 }}>
                {root.fields.map((f) => renderField(root, f, root.name))}
                <button style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 22px', padding: '2px 8px', fontSize: 10, border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1', borderRadius: 4, background: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer' }} onClick={() => addField(root.id)}>+ РїРѕР»Рµ</button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   DiagramEditor.tsx вЂ” РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№

   F-DG-01..11, F-ERR-02.
   draw.io embed-РїСЂРѕС‚РѕРєРѕР» (JSON-СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёСЏ) РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ СѓСЃС‚Р°СЂРµРІС€РёС… СЃС‚СЂРѕРє.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

interface DiagramEditorProps {
  svg: string;
  xml: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (svg: string, xml: string) => void;
}

import { CONFIG } from '../../shared/config';

const DRAWIO_URL = CONFIG.DRAWIO_URL;

export const DiagramEditor: React.FC<DiagramEditorProps> = ({
  svg: initialSvg,
  xml: initialXml,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSave,
}) => {
  const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const lastXmlRef = useRef<string>(initialXml);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
      const allowed = (() => {
        try { return event.origin === new URL(DRAWIO_URL).origin; }
        catch { return false; }
      })();
      if (!allowed) return;

      let msg: any;
      try {
        msg = typeof event.data === 'string' ? JSON.parse(event.data) : event.data;
      } catch {
        return;
      }
      if (!msg || typeof msg !== 'object') return;

      if (msg.event === 'init') {
        setIsLoading(false);
        iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage(
          JSON.stringify({ action: 'load', autosave: 1, xml: lastXmlRef.current || '<mxGraphModel/>' }),
          '*',
        );
      } else if (msg.event === 'autosave' || msg.event === 'save') {
        if (typeof msg.xml === 'string') lastXmlRef.current = msg.xml;
        iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage(
          JSON.stringify({ action: 'export', format: 'svg', xml: lastXmlRef.current }),
          '*',
        );
      } else if (msg.event === 'export') {
        const svgOut = decodeURIComponent(msg.data || msg.xml || '');
        onSave(svgOut, lastXmlRef.current);
      } else if (msg.event === 'exit') {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('message', handleMessage);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('message', handleMessage);
  }, [isOpen, onSave, onClose]);

  const handleSave = useCallback(() => {
    iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage(
      JSON.stringify({ action: 'save', xml: lastXmlRef.current }),
      '*',
    );
  }, []);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="diagram-editor-backdrop">
      <div className="diagram-editor-modal">
        <div className="diagram-editor-header">
          <h3>Diagram Editor</h3>
          <div className="diagram-editor-actions">
            <button className="diagram-btn" onClick={handleSave}>рџ’ѕ Save</button>
            <button className="diagram-btn" onClick={onClose}>вњ• Close</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="diagram-editor-body">
          {isLoading && (
            <div className="diagram-loading">
              <div className="diagram-spinner" />
              <p>Loading editor...</p>
            </div>
          )}
          <iframe
            ref={iframeRef}
            src={`${DRAWIO_URL}?embed=1&proto=json&spin=1&configure=1`}
            className="diagram-iframe"
            sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-popups allow-forms"
            allow="fullscreen"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

DiagramEditor.displayName = 'DiagramEditor';

import React from 'react';
import { DiagramEditor } from './DiagramEditor';

interface Props {
  element: DiagramElement;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (patch: Partial<DiagramElement>) => void;
}

export const DiagramEditorHost: React.FC<Props> = ({
  element,
  onClose,
  onSave,
}) => (
  <DiagramEditor
    svg={element.svg}
    xml={element.xml}
    isOpen
    onClose={onClose}
    onSave={(svg, xml) => onSave({ svg, xml })}
  />
);

import React, { useMemo, useRef, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { DocumentStore } from '../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';
import {
  EditorProvider, useDocumentStore, useModalsStore, useSelectionStore,
} from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { useAutoFitZoom } from '../hooks/useAutoFitZoom';
import { useAutoSave, loadAutoSave, clearAutoSave } from '../hooks/useAutoSave';
import { useEditorShortcuts } from '../hooks/useEditorShortcuts';
import { useEditingCommit } from '../hooks/editor/useEditingCommit';
import { useStampCommit } from '../hooks/editor/useStampCommit';
import { useDiagramCommit } from '../hooks/editor/useDiagramCommit';
import { useClipboardActions } from '../hooks/editor/useClipboardActions';
import { useStyleClipboard } from '../hooks/editor/useStyleClipboard';
import { Canvas } from './Canvas';
import { LeftPanel } from './LeftPanel';
import { RightPanel } from './RightPanel';
import { Toolbar } from './Toolbar';
import { ZoomBar } from './ZoomBar';
import { SlidesBar } from './SlidesBar';
import { ContextMenu } from './ContextMenu';
import { FloatingProps } from './FloatingProps';
import { InlineEditorHost } from './InlineEditorHost';
import { StampEditorHost } from './StampEditorHost';
import { DiagramEditorHost } from './DiagramEditorHost';
import { ErrorBoundary } from './ErrorBoundary';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

const EditorLayout: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();

  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const data = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.data);
  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);
  const editing = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.editing);
  const floatingPos = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.floatingPos);
  const contextMenu = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.contextMenu);
  const stampEditorTarget = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.stampEditorTarget);
  const diagramEditorTarget = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.diagramEditorTarget);

  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = loadAutoSave();
    if (saved && window.confirm(`Р’РѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ СЃРµСЃСЃРёСЋ РѕС‚ ${new Date(saved.savedAt).toLocaleString()}?`)) {
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        draft.slides = saved.slides;
        draft.activeId = saved.activeSlideId || saved.slides[0].id;
        draft.data = saved.data;
      }, 'restore-autosave');
      clearAutoSave();
    } else if (saved) {
      clearAutoSave();
    }
  }, [docStore]);

  const activeSlide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);

  useAutoFitZoom({
    wrapRef,
    slideWidth: activeSlide?.width ?? 0,
    slideHeight: activeSlide?.height ?? 0,
    setZoom,
  });

  useAutoSave(slides, data, activeId ?? null);
  useEditorShortcuts({ docStore, selStore, modalsStore });

  const commitEditing = useEditingCommit(docStore);
  const commitStamp = useStampCommit(docStore);
  const commitDiagram = useDiagramCommit(docStore);
  const { copy, cut, paste, duplicate } = useClipboardActions(docStore, selStore, modalsStore);
  const { copyStyle, applyStyle } = useStyleClipboard(docStore, selStore, modalsStore);

  const selectedElement = useMemo<CanvasElement | null>(
    () => activeSlide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === selectedIds[0]) ?? null,
    [activeSlide, selectedIds],
  );

  const stampElement = useMemo<CanvasElement | null>(() => {
    if (stampEditorTarget == null || !activeSlide) return null;
    return activeSlide.elements.find((e) => e.id === stampEditorTarget) ?? null;
  }, [stampEditorTarget, activeSlide]);

  const diagramElement = useMemo<CanvasElement | null>(() => {
    if (diagramEditorTarget == null || !activeSlide) return null;
    return activeSlide.elements.find((e) => e.id === diagramEditorTarget) ?? null;
  }, [diagramEditorTarget, activeSlide]);

  if (!activeSlide) return <div style={{ padding: 40 }}>РќРµС‚ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃР»Р°Р№РґР°</div>;

  const contextSelected = activeSlide.elements.filter((e) => selectedIds.includes(e.id));
  const hasStyleCopied = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard() != null;
  const canPaste = modalsStore.getClipboard().length > 0;

  const moveLayer = (dir: 1 | -1) => {
    const id = selectedIds[0];
    if (!id) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      const i = s.elements.findIndex((e) => e.id === id);
      const j = i + dir;
      if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= s.elements.length) return;
      const tmp = s.elements[i];
      s.elements[i] = s.elements[j];
      s.elements[j] = tmp;
    }, `layer-${id}`);
  };

  const deleteSelected = () => {
    if (!selectedIds.length) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => {
        if (selectedIds.includes(el.id)) return false;
        if (el.type === 'connector' && (selectedIds.includes(el.fromId) || selectedIds.includes(el.toId))) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }, 'delete');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const updateSelected = (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    if (!selectedElement) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === selectedElement.id);
      if (el) Object.assign(el, patch);
    }, `update-${selectedElement.id}`);
  };

  return (
    <div className="editor-shell">
      <Toolbar />
      <div className="editor-layout">
        <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>РћС€РёР±РєР° РІ Р»РµРІРѕР№ РїР°РЅРµР»Рё</div>}>
          <LeftPanel />
        </ErrorBoundary>

        <main className="canvas-area" ref={wrapRef}>
          <ZoomBar
            zoom={zoom}
            onChange={setZoom}
            selectedCount={selectedIds.length}
            fillMode={modalsStore.getState().fillMode}
          />
          <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>РћС€РёР±РєР° РІ С…РѕР»СЃС‚Рµ</div>}>
            <Canvas slide={activeSlide} zoom={zoom} onZoomChange={setZoom} />
          </ErrorBoundary>
        </main>

        <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>РћС€РёР±РєР° РІ РїСЂР°РІРѕР№ РїР°РЅРµР»Рё</div>}>
          <RightPanel />
        </ErrorBoundary>

        <SlidesBar />
      </div>

      {contextMenu && (
        <ContextMenu
          x={contextMenu.x} y={contextMenu.y}
          elementId={contextMenu.elementId}
          selectedElements={contextSelected}
          onClose={() => modalsStore.setContextMenu(null)}
          onCut={() => cut(contextSelected)}
          onCopy={() => copy(contextSelected)}
          onPaste={paste}
          onDuplicate={() => duplicate(contextSelected)}
          onDelete={deleteSelected}
          onBringForward={() => moveLayer(1)}
          onSendBackward={() => moveLayer(-1)}
          onCopyStyle={() => selectedElement && copyStyle(selectedElement)}
          onApplyStyle={applyStyle}
          canPaste={canPaste}
          hasStyleCopied={hasStyleCopied}
        />
      )}

      {floatingPos && selectedElement && !editing && (
        <FloatingProps
          x={floatingPos.x} y={floatingPos.y}
          element={selectedElement}
          onUpdate={updateSelected}
          onClose={() => modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null)}
          onBringForward={() => moveLayer(1)}
          onSendBackward={() => moveLayer(-1)}
          onDelete={deleteSelected}
        />
      )}

      {editing && (
        <InlineEditorHost
          key={editing.id} // <--- РљР РРўРР§Р•РЎРљР Р’РђР–РќРћ
          editing={editing}
          onClose={() => modalsStore.closeEditor()}
          onCommit={(html, text) => {
            commitEditing(editing, html, text);
            modalsStore.closeEditor();
          }}
        />
      )}

      {stampElement && stampElement.type === 'stamp' && (
        <StampEditorHost
          element={stampElement}
          onClose={() => modalsStore.closeStampEditor()}
          onSave={(patch) => {
            commitStamp(stampElement.id, patch);
            modalsStore.closeStampEditor();
          }}
        />
      )}

      {diagramElement && diagramElement.type === 'diagram' && (
        <DiagramEditorHost
          element={diagramElement}
          onClose={() => modalsStore.closeDiagramEditor()}
          onSave={(patch) => {
            commitDiagram(diagramElement.id, patch);
            modalsStore.closeDiagramEditor();
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export const Editor: React.FC = () => {
  const stores = useMemo(() => ({
    document: new DocumentStore(),
    selection: new SelectionStore(),
    modals: new ModalsStore(),
  }), []);
  return (
    <EditorProvider stores={stores}>
      <EditorLayout />
    </EditorProvider>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { getElementStrategy } from './elements/registry';

export interface ElementRendererProps {
  element: CanvasElement;
  elements: CanvasElement[];
  isSelected: boolean;
  isEditing?: boolean;
  onSelect: (e?: any) => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>, newElement?: CanvasElement) => void;
  onDblClick?: (e?: any) => void;
  onContextMenu?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragStart?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragMove?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragEnd?: (e: any) => void;
  onCellDblClick?: (r: number, c: number) => void;
}

const NOOP = () => {};

const ElementRendererInner: React.FC<ElementRendererProps> = ({
  element, elements, isSelected, isEditing, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onContextMenu, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onCellDblClick,
}) => {
  const strategy = getElementStrategy(element.type);
  if (!strategy) return null;

  const View = strategy.KonvaView;
  const dbl = onDblClick ?? NOOP;
  const ctx = onContextMenu ?? NOOP;
  const ds = onDragStart ?? NOOP;
  const dm = onDragMove ?? NOOP;
  const de = onDragEnd ?? NOOP;

  return (
    <View
      el={element} elements={elements} isSelected={isSelected} isEditing={!!isEditing}
      onSelect={onSelect} onUpdate={onUpdate} onDblClick={dbl} onContextMenu={ctx}
      onDragStart={ds} onDragMove={dm} onDragEnd={de} onCellDblClick={onCellDblClick}
      handlers={{
        onSelect, onUpdate, onDragStart: ds, onDragMove: dm,
        onDragEnd: de, onContextMenu: ctx, isSelected, onDblClick: dbl,
      }}
    />
  );
};

export const ElementRenderer = React.memo(ElementRendererInner, (prev, next) => {
  /* 
   * Р’РђР–РќРћ: РЅРµ СЃСЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РµРј callback'Рё (РѕРЅРё РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ РЅР° РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂ Canvas).
   * РЎСЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РµРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РёРјС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р°.
   */
  return (
    prev.element === next.element &&
    prev.isSelected === next.isSelected &&
    prev.isEditing === next.isEditing &&
    prev.elements === next.elements
  );
});

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Line, Text as KonvaText, Circle } from 'react-konva';
import type { ConnectorElement, CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP } from '../../../lib/stamps/render';
import {
  routeConnector, midpointOfPath, resolveConnectorEnds, findNearestAnchor,
} from '../../../lib/connectors';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: ConnectorElement;
  elements: CanvasElement[];
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<ConnectorElement>) => void;
}

export const ConnectorEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, elements, onSelect, onContextMenu, isSelected, onUpdate,
}) => {
  const { from, to } = useMemo(
    () => resolveConnectorEnds(el, elements),
    [el.fromId, el.toId, elements],
  );

  const obstacles = useMemo(
    () => elements.filter((e) =>
      e.type !== 'connector' && e.type !== 'fill' && e.type !== 'line' && !e.locked
    ),
    [elements],
  );

  const [dragState, setDragState] = useState<{ endpoint: 'from' | 'to'; x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  const points = useMemo(() => {
    if (!from || !to) return null;
    let pts = routeConnector(from, el.fromAnchor, to, el.toAnchor, el.routing, obstacles);

    if (dragState) {
      pts = [...pts];
      if (dragState.endpoint === 'from') {
        pts[0] = dragState.x;
        pts[1] = dragState.y;
      } else {
        pts[pts.length - 2] = dragState.x;
        pts[pts.length - 1] = dragState.y;
      }
    }
    return pts;
  }, [from, to, el.routing, el.fromAnchor, el.toAnchor, obstacles, dragState, from?.x, from?.y, from?.width, from?.height, to?.x, to?.y, to?.width, to?.height]);

  if (!points || !from || !to) return null;

  const dashArr = STAMP_DASH_MAP[el.dash];

  const arrowHead = useMemo(() => {
    if (!el.isArrow) return null;
    const n = points.length;
    const x2 = points[n - 2], y2 = points[n - 1];
    let i = n - 4;
    
    while (i > 0 && Math.hypot(x2 - points[i], y2 - points[i+1]) < 2) {
      i -= 2;
    }
    
    const x1 = points[i], y1 = points[i + 1];
    const angle = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
    const len = 14;
    const spread = 0.4;
    return [
      x2 + len * Math.cos(angle + Math.PI - spread), y2 + len * Math.sin(angle + Math.PI - spread),
      x2, y2,
      x2 + len * Math.cos(angle + Math.PI + spread), y2 + len * Math.sin(angle + Math.PI + spread),
    ];
  }, [el.isArrow, points]);

  const labelPos = useMemo(() => {
    if (!el.label) return null;
    const m = midpointOfPath(points);
    return { x: m.x + (el.labelOffsetX || 0), y: m.y + (el.labelOffsetY || 0) };
  }, [el.label, el.labelOffsetX, el.labelOffsetY, points]);

  const startX = points[0];
  const startY = points[1];
  const endX = points[points.length - 2];
  const endY = points[points.length - 1];

  const handleDragMove = (endpoint: 'from' | 'to', e: any) => {
    setDragState({ endpoint, x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() });
  };

  const handleDragEnd = (endpoint: 'from' | 'to', e: any) => {
    const stage = e.target.getStage();
    if (!stage) return;
    const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
    if (!pointer) { setDragState(null); return; }

    const transform = stage.getAbsoluteTransform().copy().invert();
    const pos = transform.point(pointer);
    const wx = pos.x;
    const wy = pos.y;

    const targetEl = elements.find(
      (e) => e.id !== (endpoint === 'from' ? el.toId : el.fromId) && e.type !== 'connector' && !e.locked &&
      wx >= e.x && wx <= e.x + e.width && wy >= e.y && wy <= e.y + e.height
    );

    if (targetEl) {
      const nearest = findNearestAnchor(targetEl, wx, wy);
      if (nearest.distance < 40) {
        if (endpoint === 'from') {
          onUpdate({ fromId: targetEl.id, fromAnchor: nearest.side });
        } else {
          onUpdate({ toId: targetEl.id, toAnchor: nearest.side });
        }
      }
    }
    setDragState(null);
  };

  return (
    <Group id={el.id} name="element" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }} onContextMenu={onContextMenu}>
      <Line points={points} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" strokeWidth={20} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" listening />
      <Line points={points} stroke={el.stroke} strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth} dash={dashArr} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" listening={false} />
      {arrowHead && (
        <Line points={arrowHead} stroke={el.stroke} strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" listening={false} />
      )}
      {labelPos && el.label && (
        <KonvaText x={labelPos.x} y={labelPos.y} text={el.label} fontSize={el.labelFontSize || 12} fontFamily="Arial" fill={el.labelFill || el.stroke} offsetX={el.label.length * (el.labelFontSize || 12) * 0.3} offsetY={(el.labelFontSize || 12) / 2} listening={false} />
      )}
      {isSelected && (
        <>
          <Circle x={startX} y={startY} radius={8} fill="#fbbf24" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={2} draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragStart={(e) => handleDragMove('from', e)} onDragMove={(e) => handleDragMove('from', e)} onDragEnd={(e) => handleDragEnd('from', e)} />
          <Circle x={endX} y={endY} radius={8} fill="#fbbf24" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={2} draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragStart={(e) => handleDragMove('to', e)} onDragMove={(e) => handleDragMove('to', e)} onDragEnd={(e) => handleDragEnd('to', e)} />
        </>
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { DiagramElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: DiagramElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<DiagramElement>) => void;
  onDblClick: () => void;
}

export const DiagramEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!el.svg) { setImg(null); return; }
    const blob = new Blob([el.svg], { type: 'image/svg+xml' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const image = new window.Image();
    image.onload = () => { setImg(image); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); };
    image.onerror = () => { setImg(null); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); };
    image.src = url;
  }, [el.svg]);

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(40, el.width * sx),
          height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
      {img
        ? <KImage x={0} y={0} width={el.width} height={el.height} image={img} listening={false} />
        : <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#cbd5e1" strokeWidth={1} dash={[6, 4]} />}
      {isSelected && (
        <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Circle, Line } from 'react-konva';
import type { FillElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: FillElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<FillElement>) => void;
}

export const FillEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const points = el.points || [];
  if (points.length < 6) return null;

  const updatePoint = (idx: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    const np = [...points];
    np[idx * 2] = x;
    np[idx * 2 + 1] = y;
    onUpdate({ points: np });
  };

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Line
        points={points}
        closed
        fill={el.fill}
        opacity={1}
        stroke={el.stroke || undefined}
        strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth || 0}
        listening={true}
      />
      {isSelected && points.map((_, i) => {
        if (i % 2 !== 0) return null;
        return (
          <Circle
            key={i}
            x={points[i]} y={points[i + 1]}
            radius={5} fill="#fbbf24" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={1.5} draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragMove={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; updatePoint(i / 2, e.target.x(), e.target.y()); }}
            onDragEnd={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { ImageElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: ImageElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<ImageElement>) => void;
}

export const ImageEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.src = el.src;
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
  }, [el.src]);

  if (!img) return null;

  return (
    <KImage
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} width={el.width} height={el.height} image={img} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    />
  );
};

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useEditor, EditorContent } from '@tiptap/react';
import StarterKit from '@tiptap/starter-kit';
import Underline from '@tiptap/extension-underline';
import TextAlign from '@tiptap/extension-text-align';
import TextStyle from '@tiptap/extension-text-style';
import Color from '@tiptap/extension-color';
import Highlight from '@tiptap/extension-highlight';
import Link from '@tiptap/extension-link';
import Table from '@tiptap/extension-table';
import TableRow from '@tiptap/extension-table-row';
import TableCell from '@tiptap/extension-table-cell';
import TableHeader from '@tiptap/extension-table-header';
import FontFamily from '@tiptap/extension-font-family';
import { useModalsStore, useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';

export const InlineEditor: React.FC = () => {
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const editing = modalsStore.getState().editing;

  const editor = useEditor({
    extensions: [
      StarterKit,
      Underline,
      TextAlign.configure({ types: ['heading', 'paragraph'] }),
      TextStyle,
      Color,
      Highlight.configure({ multicolor: true }),
      Link.configure({ openOnClick: false }),
      Table.configure({ resizable: true }),
      TableRow,
      TableCell,
      TableHeader,
      FontFamily,
    ],
    content: editing?.value || '<p></p>',
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (editing) setTimeout(() => editor?.commands.focus('end'), 50);
  }, [editing, editor]);

  if (!editing) return null;

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!editor) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === editing.id);
      if (el && el.type === 'text') {
        el.text = editor.getText();
        el.html = editor.getHTML();
      }
    }, `update-text-${editing.id}`);
    modalsStore.closeEditor();
  };

  return (
    <div className="rich-modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); modalsStore.closeEditor(); }}>
      <div className="rich-modal" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} style={{ width: '90vw', maxWidth: 900, height: '80vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', background: '#fff', borderRadius: 8 }}>
        <div style={{ padding: 10, borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', display: 'flex', gap: 4, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()} className={editor?.isActive('bold') ? 'active' : ''}><b>B</b></button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()} className={editor?.isActive('italic') ? 'active' : ''}><i>I</i></button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleUnderline().run()} className={editor?.isActive('underline') ? 'active' : ''}><u>U</u></button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()} className={editor?.isActive('strike') ? 'active' : ''}><s>S</s></button>
          <span style={{ width: 1, background: '#eee', margin: '0 4px' }} />
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 1 }).run()} className={editor?.isActive('heading', { level: 1 }) ? 'active' : ''}>H1</button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 2 }).run()} className={editor?.isActive('heading', { level: 2 }) ? 'active' : ''}>H2</button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 3 }).run()} className={editor?.isActive('heading', { level: 3 }) ? 'active' : ''}>H3</button>
          <span style={{ width: 1, background: '#eee', margin: '0 4px' }} />
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleBulletList().run()} className={editor?.isActive('bulletList') ? 'active' : ''}>вЂў List</button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().toggleOrderedList().run()} className={editor?.isActive('orderedList') ? 'active' : ''}>1. List</button>
          <span style={{ width: 1, background: '#eee', margin: '0 4px' }} />
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().setTextAlign('left').run()}>в†ђ</button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().setTextAlign('center').run()}>в†”</button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().setTextAlign('right').run()}>в†’</button>
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().setTextAlign('justify').run()}>в‰Ў</button>
          <span style={{ width: 1, background: '#eee', margin: '0 4px' }} />
          <button onClick={() => editor?.chain().focus().insertTable({ rows: 3, cols: 3, withHeaderRow: true }).run()}>+ Table</button>
          <div style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          <button onClick={() => modalsStore.closeEditor()} style={{ padding: '4px 12px' }}>РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
          <button onClick={handleSave} style={{ padding: '4px 12px', background: '#6366f1', color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 4 }}>РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
        </div>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: 20 }}>
          <EditorContent editor={editor} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: presentation\components\elements\LineEl.tsx
SIZE: 2866 bytes

import React from 'react';
import { Group } from 'react-konva';
import type { LineElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import { PolylineRenderer } from '../shapes/PolylineRenderer';
import { ensureMinimalPoints } from '../../../lib/polyline';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: LineElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<LineElement>) => void;
  onDblClick?: (stageX: number, stageY: number) => void;
}

export const LineEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const points = ensureMinimalPoints(el.points as any, el.width, el.height);

  const handleDblClick = (e: any) => {
    e.cancelBubble = true;
    if (!onDblClick) return;
    const stage = e.target.getStage();
    const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
    if (!p || !stage) return;
    onDblClick(p.x / stage.scaleX(), p.y / stage.scaleY());
  };

  const handlePointMove = (index: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    const np = [...points];
    np[index * 2] = x;
    np[index * 2 + 1] = y;
    onUpdate({ points: np });
  };

  const handleTransformEnd = (e: any) => {
    const n = e.target;
    const sx = n.scaleX();
    const sy = n.scaleY();
    n.scaleX(1);
    n.scaleY(1);

    const scaledPoints = points.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v * sx : v * sy));

    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < scaledPoints.length; i += 2) {
      const px = scaledPoints[i], py = scaledPoints[i + 1];
      if (px < minX) minX = px;
      if (py < minY) minY = py;
      if (px > maxX) maxX = px;
      if (py > maxY) maxY = py;
    }
    const newW = Math.max(10, maxX - minX);
    const newH = Math.max(10, maxY - minY);

    onUpdate({
      x: n.x(),
      y: n.y(),
      rotation: n.rotation(),
      width: newW,
      height: newH,
      points: scaledPoints,
    });
  };

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onDblClick={handleDblClick}
      onDblTap={handleDblClick}
      onTransformEnd={handleTransformEnd}
    >
      <PolylineRenderer
        points={points}
        stroke={el.stroke}
        strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth}
        dash={el.dash}
        isArrow={el.isArrow}
        isSelected={!!isSelected}
        onPointMove={handlePointMove}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { PlaceholderElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: PlaceholderElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<PlaceholderElement>) => void;
}

export const PlaceholderEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  const textRef = useRef<Konva.Text>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (el.fieldType !== 'image' || !el.sample) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.src = el.sample;
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
    i.onerror = () => setImg(null);
  }, [el.sample, el.fieldType]);

  const pad = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerW = Math.max(10, el.width - pad.left - pad.right);
  const pathLabel = `{{${el.fieldPath}}}`;
  const sampleText = el.sample || 'вЂ”';

  const konvaStyle = [
    (el.fontStyle || '').includes('bold') ? 'bold' : '',
    (el.fontStyle || '').includes('italic') ? 'italic' : '',
  ].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || 'normal';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (el.fieldType === 'image') return;
    if (el.inline) return;
    const node = textRef.current;
    if (!node) return;
    const measured = Math.ceil(node.height() + pad.top + pad.bottom + 24);
    if (Math.abs(measured - el.height) > 2) onUpdate({ height: measured });
  }, [el.sample, el.fontSize, el.width, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle, el.lineHeight, el.align, el.padding, el.fieldType, el.inline]);

  if (el.inline) {
    if (el.fieldType === 'image') {
      return (
        <Group
          id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
          onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
          onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
          onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
          onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
          onTransformEnd={(e) => {
            const n = e.target;
            const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
            n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
            onUpdate({
              x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
              width: Math.max(20, el.width * sx),
              height: Math.max(20, el.height * sy),
            });
          }}
        >
          <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
          {img ? <KImage x={0} y={0} width={el.width} height={el.height} image={img} listening={false} />
               : <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="#f0f0f0" listening={false} />}
          {isSelected && (
            <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height}
              stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />
          )}
        </Group>
      );
    }
    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
        onTransformEnd={(e) => {
          const n = e.target;
          const sx = n.scaleX();
          n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
          onUpdate({
            x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
            width: Math.max(20, el.width * sx),
          });
        }}
      >
        {el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' && (
          <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill={el.background} />
        )}
        <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
        {isSelected && (
          <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height}
            stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />
        )}
        <Text
          ref={textRef}
          x={pad.left} y={pad.top}
          width={innerW}
          text={sampleText}
          fontSize={el.fontSize}
          fontFamily={el.fontFamily || 'Arial'}
          fontStyle={konvaStyle}
          textDecoration={el.textDecoration || ''}
          align={el.align}
          lineHeight={el.lineHeight || 1.2}
          fill={el.fill}
          wrap="word"
          listening={false}
        />
      </Group>
    );
  }

  if (el.fieldType === 'image') {
    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
        onTransformEnd={(e) => {
          const n = e.target;
          const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
          n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
          onUpdate({
            x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
            width: Math.max(40, el.width * sx),
            height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
          });
        }}
      >
        <Rect
          width={el.width} height={el.height}
          fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} cornerRadius={6}
        />
        <Rect width={el.width} height={22} fill="#6366f1" cornerRadius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />
        <Text
          x={8} y={6} width={el.width - 16}
          text={`рџ–ј ${pathLabel}`}
          fontSize={11} fontFamily="monospace" fill="#ffffff" listening={false}
        />
        {img ? (
          <KImage
            x={pad.left} y={22 + pad.top}
            width={innerW}
            height={Math.max(10, el.height - 22 - pad.top - pad.bottom)}
            image={img}
            listening={false}
          />
        ) : (
          <Text
            x={pad.left} y={(el.height + 22) / 2} width={innerW}
            align="center"
            text="(РєР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєР° РЅРµ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶РµРЅР°)"
            fontSize={12} fill="#94a3b8" listening={false}
          />
        )}
      </Group>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(60, el.width * sx),
        });
      }}
    >
      <Rect
        width={el.width} height={el.height}
        fill={el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' ? el.background : '#f8fafc'}
        stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} cornerRadius={6}
      />
      <Rect width={el.width} height={20} fill="#6366f1" cornerRadius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />
      <Text
        x={8} y={4} width={el.width - 16}
        text={pathLabel}
        fontSize={11} fontFamily="monospace" fill="#ffffff" listening={false}
      />
      <Text
        ref={textRef}
        x={pad.left} y={20 + pad.top}
        width={innerW}
        text={sampleText}
        fontSize={el.fontSize}
        fontFamily={el.fontFamily || 'Arial'}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle}
        textDecoration={el.textDecoration || ''}
        align={el.align}
        verticalAlign={el.verticalAlign || 'top'}
        lineHeight={el.lineHeight || 1.2}
        fill={el.fill}
        wrap="word"
        listening={false}
      />
    </Group>

import React from 'react';

  LineElement, ImageElement, FooterElement, PlaceholderElement, 
  FillElement, ConnectorElement, StampElement, DiagramElement 
} from '../../../../shared/types';
import { Group, L, inputStyle, normalizeColor } from './ui';
import { useModalsStore } from '../../../providers/EditorProvider';

export const LineProps: React.FC<{ el: LineElement; update: (p: Partial<LineElement>) => void }> = ({ el, update }) => (

import React from 'react';
import type { TableElement } from '../../../../shared/types';
import { Group, L, inputStyle, normalizeColor } from './ui';

export const TableProps: React.FC<{ el: TableElement; update: (p: Partial<TableElement>) => void }> = ({ el, update }) => (
  <Group title="РўР°Р±Р»РёС†Р°">
    <L label="РЎС‚СЂРѕРє"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" min={1} value={el.rows} onChange={(e) => {
      const rows = Math.max(1, +e.target.value);
      const data = el.data.slice(0, rows);
      while (data.length < rows) data.push(Array(el.cols).fill(''));
      update({ rows, data });
    }} /></L>
    <L label="РљРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" min={1} value={el.cols} onChange={(e) => {
      const cols = Math.max(1, +e.target.value);
      const data = el.data.map((r) => {
        const nr = r.slice(0, cols);
        while (nr.length < cols) nr.push('');
        return nr;
      });
      update({ cols, data });
    }} /></L>
    <L label="Р¦РІРµС‚ РіСЂР°РЅРёС†"><input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.borderColor)} onChange={(e) => update({ borderColor: e.target.value })} /></L>
    <L label="Р¦РІРµС‚ С€Р°РїРєРё"><input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.headerBg)} onChange={(e) => update({ headerBg: e.target.value })} /></L>
  </Group>
);

import React from 'react';

export function normalizeColor(c: string | undefined | null): string {
  if (!c || c === 'transparent') return '#ffffff';
  if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(c)) {
    return '#' + c[1] + c[1] + c[2] + c[2] + c[3] + c[3];
  }
  return c;
}

export const inputStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: '100%', padding: '5px 8px', fontSize: 12,
  border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: 4, boxSizing: 'border-box',
};

export const Group: React.FC<{ title: string; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ title, children }) => (
  <div style={{ marginBottom: 12, paddingBottom: 12, borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
    <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 8px', fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>{title}</h4>
    {children}
  </div>
);

export const L: React.FC<{ label: string; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ label, children }) => (
  <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b', marginBottom: 4 }}>
    <span style={{ display: 'block', marginBottom: 2 }}>{label}</span>
    {children}
  </label>
);

import { registerElement } from './registry';

// View РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚С‹
import { TextEl } from './TextEl';
import { ShapeEl } from './ShapeEl';
import { LineEl } from './LineEl';
import { TableEl } from './TableEl';
import { FooterEl } from './FooterEl';
import { ImageEl } from './ImageEl';
import { PlaceholderEl } from './PlaceholderEl';
import { FillEl } from './FillEl';
import { ConnectorEl } from './ConnectorEl';
import { DiagramEl } from './DiagramEl';
import { StampEl } from './StampEl';

// РџР°РЅРµР»Рё СЃРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІ
import { TextProps } from './properties/TextProps';
import { ShapeProps } from './properties/ShapeProps';
import { TableProps } from './properties/TableProps';
import { LineProps, ImageProps, FooterProps, PlaceholderProps, FillProps, ConnectorProps, StampProps, DiagramProps } from './properties/MiscProps';

// SVG Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚РµСЂС‹
import { 
  toSvgText, toSvgShape, toSvgLine, toSvgTable, toSvgImage, 
  toSvgFooter, toSvgPlaceholder, toSvgFill, toSvgConnector, 
  toSvgDiagram, toSvgStamp 
} from '../../../lib/svg/exporters';

export function registerAllElements(): void {
  registerElement({ type: 'text', KonvaView: TextEl, PropertiesPanel: TextProps as any, toSvg: toSvgText });
  registerElement({ type: 'shape', KonvaView: ShapeEl, PropertiesPanel: ShapeProps as any, toSvg: toSvgShape });
  registerElement({ type: 'line', KonvaView: LineEl, PropertiesPanel: LineProps as any, toSvg: toSvgLine });
  registerElement({ type: 'table', KonvaView: TableEl, PropertiesPanel: TableProps as any, toSvg: toSvgTable });
  registerElement({ type: 'footer', KonvaView: FooterEl, PropertiesPanel: FooterProps as any, toSvg: toSvgFooter });
  registerElement({ type: 'image', KonvaView: ImageEl, PropertiesPanel: ImageProps as any, toSvg: toSvgImage });
  registerElement({ type: 'placeholder', KonvaView: PlaceholderEl, PropertiesPanel: PlaceholderProps as any, toSvg: toSvgPlaceholder });
  registerElement({ type: 'fill', KonvaView: FillEl, PropertiesPanel: FillProps as any, toSvg: toSvgFill });
  registerElement({ 
    type: 'connector', 
    KonvaView: ConnectorEl, 
    PropertiesPanel: ConnectorProps as any, 
    toSvg: (el, ctx) => toSvgConnector(el, ctx?.allElements || []) 
  });
  registerElement({ type: 'diagram', KonvaView: DiagramEl, PropertiesPanel: DiagramProps as any, toSvg: toSvgDiagram });
  registerElement({ type: 'stamp', KonvaView: StampEl, PropertiesPanel: StampProps as any, toSvg: toSvgStamp });
}

import React from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';

export interface CommonHandlers {
  onSelect: (e?: any) => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: any) => void;
  onDragStart: (e: any) => void;
  onDragMove: (e: any) => void;
  onDragEnd: (e: any) => void;
  onContextMenu: (e: any) => void;
  isSelected: boolean;
  onDblClick?: (e?: any) => void;
}

export interface ElementViewProps {
  el: CanvasElement;
  handlers: CommonHandlers;
  elements: CanvasElement[];
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

export interface ElementStrategy<T extends CanvasElement = CanvasElement> {
  type: T['type'];
  KonvaView: React.FC<any>;
  PropertiesPanel?: React.FC<{ el: T; update: (p: Partial<T>) => void }>; // <--- РќРћР’РћР•
  toSvg: (el: T, ctx?: { allElements: CanvasElement[] }) => string;
}

const registry = new Map<string, ElementStrategy>();

export function registerElement<T extends CanvasElement>(strategy: ElementStrategy<T>): void {
  registry.set(strategy.type, strategy as ElementStrategy);
}

export function getElementStrategy(type: string): ElementStrategy | null {
  return registry.get(type) ?? null;
}
