import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Stage, Layer, Rect, Circle, Ellipse, Line,
  Text as KonvaText, Shape as KonvaShape, Image as KonvaImage, Transformer,
} from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampElement, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';
import { useStampFillMode } from '../hooks/useStampFillMode';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from './stamps/CompositeFillLayer';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP, konvaStyle } from '../../lib/stamps/render';

interface StampEditorProps {
  stamp: StampElement;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (stamp: StampElement) => void;
}

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image';
interface Snapshot { shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[] }

const SNAP_TO_GRID = 10;
const GRID_SNAP_DIST = 2;
const MIN_SIZE = 8;
const HISTORY_LIMIT = 60;

export const StampEditor: React.FC<StampEditorProps> = ({ stamp, isOpen, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const [shapes, setShapes] = useState<StampShapeItem[]>(stamp.shapes ?? []);
  const [texts, setTexts] = useState<StampTextItem[]>(stamp.texts ?? []);
  const [images, setImages] = useState<StampImageItem[]>(stamp.images ?? []);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<SelectionType | null>(null);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [gridOn, setGridOn] = useState(true);

  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);
  const historyRef = useRef<Snapshot[]>([]);
  const futureRef = useRef<Snapshot[]>([]);

  const pushHistory = () => {
    historyRef.current.push({
      shapes: shapes.map((s) => ({ ...s })),
      texts: texts.map((t) => ({ ...t })),
      images: images.map((im) => ({ ...im })),
    });
    if (historyRef.current.length > HISTORY_LIMIT) historyRef.current.shift();
    futureRef.current = [];
  };

  const setFullState = (next: Snapshot) => {
    setShapes(next.shapes);
    setTexts(next.texts);
    setImages(next.images);
  };

  const undo = () => {
    const prev = historyRef.current.pop();
    if (!prev) return;
    futureRef.current.push({ shapes, texts, images });
    setFullState(prev);
  };

  const redo = () => {
    const next = futureRef.current.pop();
    if (!next) return;
    historyRef.current.push({ shapes, texts, images });
    setFullState(next);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    setShapes(stamp.shapes ?? []);
    setTexts(stamp.texts ?? []);
    setImages(stamp.images ?? []);
    setSelectedId(null);
    setSelectedType(null);
    setZoom(1);
    historyRef.current = [];
    futureRef.current = [];
  }, [isOpen, stamp]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!tr || !stage) return;
    const isFill = shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId)?.kind === 'fill';
    if (!selectedId || isFill) { tr.nodes([]); tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw(); return; }
    const node = stage.findOne('#' + selectedId);
    if (node) tr.nodes([node]);
    tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedId, shapes, texts, images]);

  const handleWheel = (e: any) => {
    e.evt.preventDefault();
    const dir = e.evt.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1;
    setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.4, Math.min(6, z + dir * 0.08)));
  };

  const patchShape = (id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => {
    pushHistory();
    setShapes((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...patch } : s)));
  };
  const patchText = (id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => {
    pushHistory();
    setTexts((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, ...patch } : t)));
  };
  const patchImage = (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => {
    pushHistory();
    setImages((prev) => prev.map((im) => (im.id === id ? { ...im, ...patch } : im)));
  };

  const addShape = (kind: StampShapeItem['kind']) => {
    const isCircle = kind === 'circle';
    const w = isCircle ? 120 : kind === 'rect' ? 160 : 140;
    const h = isCircle ? 120 : kind === 'rect' ? 100 : 120;
    const s: StampShapeItem = {
      id: generateId(), kind,
      x: Math.round((stamp.width - w) / 2),
      y: Math.round((stamp.height - h) / 2),
      width: w, height: h,
      cornerRadius: kind === 'rect' ? 12 : 0,
      stroke: '#000', strokeWidth: 2, dash: 'solid',
    };
    pushHistory();
    setShapes((prev) => [...prev, s]);
    setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape');
  };

  const addText = (mode: 'straight' | 'arc') => {
    const t: StampTextItem = {
      id: generateId(),
      text: mode === 'arc' ? 'РўР•РљРЎРў РџРћ Р”РЈР“Р•' : 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚',
      mode,
      x: Math.round(stamp.width / 2 - 75),
      y: Math.round(stamp.height / 2 - 10),
      width: 150, align: 'center',
      arcCx: stamp.width / 2, arcCy: stamp.height / 2,
      arcRadius: Math.min(stamp.width, stamp.height) / 2 - 8,
      arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
      arcOffset: 6, arcSpanDeg: 140,
      fontSize: 16, fontFamily: 'Arial',
      fontStyle: 'bold', textDecoration: '', fill: '#000',
    };
    pushHistory();
    setTexts((prev) => [...prev, t]);
    setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text');
  };

  const deleteSelected = () => {
    if (!selectedId) return;
    pushHistory();
    if (selectedType === 'shape') setShapes((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== selectedId));
    if (selectedType === 'text') setTexts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== selectedId));
    if (selectedType === 'image') setImages((prev) => prev.filter((im) => im.id !== selectedId));
    setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null);
  };

  const fillApi = useStampFillMode({
    shapes, texts, images,
    width: stamp.width, height: stamp.height,
    commit: (s, t, i) => { pushHistory(); setShapes(s); setTexts(t); setImages(i); },
    select: (id, type) => { setSelectedId(id); setSelectedType(id ? (type as SelectionType) : null); },
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
      const tag = (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null)?.tagName;
      const isField = tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA';
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        if (isField) return;
        e.preventDefault(); deleteSelected();
      } else if (ctrl && e.code === 'KeyZ' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); undo(); }
      else if (ctrl && (e.code === 'KeyY' || (e.code === 'KeyZ' && e.shiftKey))) { e.preventDefault(); redo(); }
      else if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [isOpen, shapes, texts, images, selectedId, selectedType]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const selectedShape = selectedType === 'shape' ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) : null;
  const selectedText = selectedType === 'text' ? texts.find((t) => t.id === selectedId) : null;
  const selectedImage = selectedType === 'image' ? images.find((im) => im.id === selectedId) : null;

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-backdrop">
      <div className="stamp-editor-modal">
        <div className="stamp-editor-head">
          <input type="text" defaultValue="Custom Stamp" />
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
            <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={undo}>в†¶</button>
            <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={redo}>в†·</button>
            <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={onClose}>РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
            <button className="stamp-btn primary" onClick={() => onSave({ ...stamp, shapes, texts, images })}>РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="stamp-toolbar">
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('circle')}>в—Ї РљСЂСѓРі</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('ellipse')}>в¬­ Р­Р»Р»РёРїСЃ</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('rect')}>в–­ РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРі.</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('triangle')}>в–і РўСЂРµСѓРі.</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('line')}>в•± Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addText('straight')}>T РўРµРєСЃС‚</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addText('arc')}>в‰ Р”СѓРіР°</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className={`stamp-btn ${fillApi.fillMode ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => fillApi.setFillMode(!fillApi.fillMode)}>рџ–Њ Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</button>
          {fillApi.fillMode && (
            <input type="color" value={fillApi.fillColor} onChange={(e) => fillApi.setFillColor(e.target.value)} style={{ width: 32, height: 26 }} />
          )}
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => setGridOn((v) => !v)}>{gridOn ? 'в‘' : 'вђ'} РЎРµС‚РєР°</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(6, z + 0.2))}>+</button>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 12, width: 40, textAlign: 'center' }}>{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.4, z - 0.2))}>в€’</button>
        </div>

        <div className="stamp-editor-body">
          <div className="stamp-editor-left">
            <div className="stamp-layers-head">РЎР»РѕРё</div>
            <div className="stamp-layers-list">
              {texts.map((t) => (
                <div key={t.id} className={`stamp-layer ${selectedId === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-icon">T</span>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-name">{t.text || '(РїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ)'}</span>
                  <button className="stamp-layer-btn" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); pushHistory(); setTexts((p) => p.filter((x) => x.id !== t.id)); }}>вњ•</button>
                </div>
              ))}
              {images.map((im) => (
                <div key={im.id} className={`stamp-layer ${selectedId === im.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => { setSelectedId(im.id); setSelectedType('image'); }}>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-icon">рџ–ј</span>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-name">{im.name || 'Image'}</span>
                  <button className="stamp-layer-btn" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); pushHistory(); setImages((p) => p.filter((x) => x.id !== im.id)); }}>вњ•</button>
                </div>
              ))}
              {shapes.map((s) => (
                <div key={s.id} className={`stamp-layer ${selectedId === s.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => { setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape'); }}>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-icon">
                    {s.kind === 'circle' ? 'в—Ї' : s.kind === 'ellipse' ? 'в¬­' : s.kind === 'rect' ? 'в–­' : s.kind === 'triangle' ? 'в–і' : s.kind === 'line' ? 'в•±' : 'рџЋЁ'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-name">{s.name || s.kind}</span>
                  <button className="stamp-layer-btn" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); pushHistory(); setShapes((p) => p.filter((x) => x.id !== s.id)); }}>вњ•</button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="stamp-editor-canvas">
            <Stage
              ref={stageRef}
              width={stamp.width * zoom}
              height={stamp.height * zoom}
              scaleX={zoom} scaleY={zoom}
              onWheel={handleWheel}
              onMouseDown={(e) => {
                if (fillApi.fillMode && e.target === e.target.getStage()) {
                  const pos = e.target.getStage()?.getRelativePointerPosition();
                  if (pos) fillApi.handleCanvasClick(pos.x, pos.y);
                } else if (e.target === e.target.getStage()) {
                  setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null);
                }
              }}
              style={{ background: '#fff', boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)' }}
            >
              <Layer>
                <Rect width={stamp.width} height={stamp.height} fill="#fff" listening={false} />
                {gridOn && (
                  <KonvaShape
                    listening={false}
                    sceneFunc={(ctx, shape) => {
                      const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
                      c.beginPath();
                      for (let x = 0; x <= stamp.width; x += 10) { c.moveTo(x, 0); c.lineTo(x, stamp.height); }
                      for (let y = 0; y <= stamp.height; y += 10) { c.moveTo(0, y); c.lineTo(stamp.width, y); }
                      c.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7';
                      c.lineWidth = 1;
                      c.stroke();
                      ctx.strokeShape(shape);
                    }}
                  />
                )}

                <CompositeFillLayer width={stamp.width} height={stamp.height} shapes={shapes} />

                {shapes.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill').map((s) => (
                  <ShapeNode
                    key={s.id}
                    shape={s}
                    draggable={!fillApi.fillMode}
                    snapEnabled={!fillApi.fillMode}
                    onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape'); }}
                    onPatch={(p) => patchShape(s.id, p)}
                  />
                ))}

                {images.map((im) => (
                  <ImageNode
                    key={im.id}
                    im={im}
                    draggable={!fillApi.fillMode}
                    onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(im.id); setSelectedType('image'); }}
                    onPatch={(p) => patchImage(im.id, p)}
                  />
                ))}

                {texts.map((t) => (
                  <TextNode
                    key={t.id}
                    t={t}
                    draggable={!fillApi.fillMode}
                    onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}
                    onPatch={(p) => patchText(t.id, p)}
                  />
                ))}

                {!fillApi.fillMode && (
                  <Transformer
                    ref={trRef}
                    rotateEnabled
                    keepRatio={false}
                    anchorSize={8}
                    onTransformEnd={(e) => {
                      const node = e.target as Konva.Node;
                      const id = node.id();
                      const sx = node.scaleX();
                      const sy = node.scaleY();
                      node.scaleX(1); node.scaleY(1);

                      const shp = shapes.find((s) => s.id === id);
                      if (shp) {
                        patchShape(id, {
                          x: Math.round(node.x() - (shp.width * sx) / 2),
                          y: Math.round(node.y() - (shp.height * sy) / 2),
                          width: Math.max(MIN_SIZE, Math.round(shp.width * sx)),
                          height: Math.max(MIN_SIZE, Math.round(shp.height * sy)),
                          rotation: Math.round(node.rotation()),
                        });
                        return;
                      }
                      const txt = texts.find((t) => t.id === id);
                      if (txt) {
                        patchText(id, {
                          x: Math.round(node.x()),
                          y: Math.round(node.y()),
                          width: Math.max(20, Math.round((txt.width || 200) * sx)),
                          rotation: Math.round(node.rotation()),
                        });
                        return;
                      }
                      const img = images.find((im) => im.id === id);
                      if (img) {
                        patchImage(id, {
                          x: Math.round(node.x() - (img.width * sx) / 2),
                          y: Math.round(node.y() - (img.height * sy) / 2),
                          width: Math.max(MIN_SIZE, Math.round(img.width * sx)),
                          height: Math.max(MIN_SIZE, Math.round(img.height * sy)),
                          rotation: Math.round(node.rotation()),
                        });
                      }
                    }}
                  />
                )}
              </Layer>
            </Stage>
          </div>

          <div className="stamp-editor-right">
            {selectedShape && (
              <>
                <h4>РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹</h4>
                <label>РћР±РІРѕРґРєР° <input type="color" value={selectedShape.stroke || '#000000'} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { stroke: e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>РўРѕР»С‰РёРЅР° <input type="number" min={0} value={selectedShape.strokeWidth} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° <input type="color" value={selectedShape.fill || '#ffffff'} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>РЎРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ <input type="number" min={0} value={selectedShape.cornerRadius} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { cornerRadius: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>РЈРіРѕР» <input type="number" value={selectedShape.rotation ?? 0} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { rotation: +e.target.value })} /></label>
              </>
            )}
            {selectedText && (
              <>
                <h4>РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°</h4>
                <label>РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ
                  <textarea value={selectedText.text} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { text: e.target.value })} rows={3} />
                </label>
                <label>Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ <input type="number" value={selectedText.fontSize} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { fontSize: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>Р¦РІРµС‚ <input type="color" value={selectedText.fill} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
                {selectedText.mode === 'arc' && (
                  <>
                    <h4 style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>Р”СѓРіР°</h4>
                    <label>Р Р°РґРёСѓСЃ <input type="number" value={selectedText.arcRadius} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { arcRadius: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                    <label>РќР°С‡Р°Р»Рѕ (В°) <input type="number" value={selectedText.arcStartDeg} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { arcStartDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                    <label>РљРѕРЅРµС† (В°) <input type="number" value={selectedText.arcEndDeg} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { arcEndDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                  </>
                )}
              </>
            )}
            {selectedImage && (
              <>
                <h4>РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ</h4>
                <img src={selectedImage.src} alt="" style={{ width: '100%', maxHeight: 120, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
              </>
            )}
            {!selectedShape && !selectedText && !selectedImage && (
              <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 40 }}>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   Sub-components
   ============================================================ */

interface ShapeNodeProps {
  shape: StampShapeItem;
  draggable: boolean;
  snapEnabled: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onPatch: (p: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => void;
}

const ShapeNode: React.FC<ShapeNodeProps> = ({ shape, draggable, snapEnabled, onSelect, onPatch }) => {
  const cx = shape.x + shape.width / 2;
  const cy = shape.y + shape.height / 2;

  const common = {
    stroke: shape.stroke,
    strokeWidth: shape.strokeWidth,
    dash: STAMP_DASH_MAP[shape.dash],
    fill: shape.fill || 'transparent',
    listening: true,
    draggable,
    onClick: onSelect,
    onTap: onSelect,
    onDragEnd: (e: any) => {
      let nx = e.target.x() - shape.width / 2;
      let ny = e.target.y() - shape.height / 2;
      if (snapEnabled && !e.evt?.ctrlKey && !e.evt?.metaKey) {
        const gx = Math.round(nx / SNAP_TO_GRID) * SNAP_TO_GRID;
        const gy = Math.round(ny / SNAP_TO_GRID) * SNAP_TO_GRID;
        if (Math.abs(gx - nx) <= GRID_SNAP_DIST) nx = gx;
        if (Math.abs(gy - ny) <= GRID_SNAP_DIST) ny = gy;
      }
      onPatch({ x: Math.round(nx), y: Math.round(ny) });
    },
  };

  switch (shape.kind) {
    case 'circle':
      return <Circle id={shape.id} name="stamp-element" x={cx} y={cy} radius={Math.min(shape.width, shape.height) / 2} {...common} />;
    case 'ellipse':
      return <Ellipse id={shape.id} name="stamp-element" x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={shape.width / 2} radiusY={shape.height / 2} {...common} />;
    case 'rect':
      return (
        <Rect
          id={shape.id}
          name="stamp-element"
          x={cx}
          y={cy}
          offsetX={shape.width / 2}
          offsetY={shape.height / 2}
          width={shape.width}
          height={shape.height}
          cornerRadius={shape.cornerRadius || 0}
          {...common}
        />
      );
    case 'triangle':
      return (
        <Line
          id={shape.id}
          name="stamp-element"
          points={[cx, shape.y, shape.x + shape.width, shape.y + shape.height, shape.x, shape.y + shape.height]}
          closed
          {...common}
        />
      );
    case 'line':
      return (
        <Line
          id={shape.id}
          name="stamp-element"
          x={shape.x}
          y={shape.y}
          points={shape.points && shape.points.length >= 4 ? shape.points.slice() : [0, shape.height / 2, shape.width, shape.height / 2]}
          {...common}
        />
      );
    default:
      return null;
  }
};

interface TextNodeProps {
  t: StampTextItem;
  draggable: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onPatch: (p: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
}

const TextNode: React.FC<TextNodeProps> = ({ t, draggable, onSelect, onPatch }) => {
  if (t.mode === 'straight') {
    return (
      <KonvaText
        id={t.id}
        name="stamp-element"
        x={t.x} y={t.y} width={t.width}
        text={t.text}
        fontSize={t.fontSize}
        fontFamily={t.fontFamily}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle(t.fontStyle)}
        textDecoration={t.textDecoration}
        fill={t.fill}
        align={t.align}
        draggable={draggable}
        onClick={onSelect}
        onTap={onSelect}
        onDragEnd={(e) => onPatch({ x: Math.round(e.target.x()), y: Math.round(e.target.y()) })}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <KonvaShape
      id={t.id}
      name="stamp-element"
      draggable={draggable}
      onClick={onSelect}
      onTap={onSelect}
      onDragEnd={(e) => onPatch({ x: Math.round(e.target.x()), y: Math.round(e.target.y()) })}
      sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
        const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        const parts: string[] = [];
        if (t.fontStyle.includes('italic')) parts.push('italic');
        if (t.fontStyle.includes('bold')) parts.push('bold');
        parts.push(`${t.fontSize}px`, `${t.fontFamily}, Arial`);
        c.font = parts.join(' ');
        c.fillStyle = t.fill;
        c.textAlign = 'center';
        c.textBaseline = 'middle';
        const chars = Array.from(t.text);
        const start = (t.arcStartDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
        const end = (t.arcEndDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
        const step = chars.length > 1 ? (end - start) / (chars.length - 1) : 0;
        const rx = t.arcRx ?? t.arcRadius;
        const ry = t.arcRy ?? t.arcRadius;
        chars.forEach((ch, i) => {
          const a = start + i * step;
          const x = t.arcCx + Math.cos(a) * rx;
          const y = t.arcCy + Math.sin(a) * ry;
          const tx = -rx * Math.sin(a);
          const ty = ry * Math.cos(a);
          const rot = Math.atan2(ty, tx);
          c.save();
          c.translate(x, y);
          c.rotate(rot);
          c.fillText(ch, 0, 0);
          c.restore();
        });
      }}
    />
  );
};

interface ImageNodeProps {
  im: StampImageItem;
  draggable: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onPatch: (p: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void;
}

const ImageNode: React.FC<ImageNodeProps> = ({ im, draggable, onSelect, onPatch }) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!im.src) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
    i.onerror = () => setImg(null);
    i.src = im.src;
  }, [im.src]);
  if (!img) return null;
  return (
    <KonvaImage
      id={im.id}
      name="stamp-element"
      x={im.x} y={im.y}
      width={im.width} height={im.height}
      image={img}
      draggable={draggable}
      onClick={onSelect}
      onTap={onSelect}
      onDragEnd={(e) => onPatch({ x: Math.round(e.target.x()), y: Math.round(e.target.y()) })}
    />
  );
};

export const StampLibraryPanel: React.FC = () => {

  };

      mode: 'create',

        id: generateId(),
        type: 'stamp',
        x: 200,
        y: 200,
        width: 200,
        height: 200,
        rotation: 0,
        shapes: [],
        texts: [],
        images: [],
      },
    });
  };

    setEditing({
      mode: 'edit',
      sourceId: tpl.id,
      sourceIsBuiltin: builtin,

        id: generateId(),

        y: 200,
        width: tpl.width,
        height: tpl.height,
        rotation: 0,
        shapes: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.shapes)),
        texts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.texts)),
        images: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.images || [])),
      },
    });
  };

  /* В§F-SLIB-05..08 */
  const handleSave = (saved: StampElement) => {
    if (!editing) return;
    const shapes = saved.shapes ?? [];
    const texts = saved.texts ?? [];
    const images = saved.images ?? [];

    if (editing.mode === 'create') {
      const tpl: StampTemplate = {
        id: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
        name: saved.name || 'РњРѕР№ С€С‚Р°РјРї',
        width: saved.width,
        height: saved.height,
        shapes: [...shapes],
        texts: [...texts],
        images: [...images],
        custom: true,
      };
      addCustomStamp(tpl);
      addElement({ ...newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200) });
    } else if (editing.sourceIsBuiltin) {
      /* В§F-SLIB-08: builtin РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ вЂ” С„РѕСЂРєР°РµРј РІ custom. */
      const source = stamps.find((s) => s.id === editing.sourceId);
      if (source) {
        const copy = forkStampAsCustom({
          ...source,
          width: saved.width,
          height: saved.height,
          shapes: [...shapes],
          texts: [...texts],
          images: [...images],
        });
        addCustomStamp(copy);
        addElement({ ...newStampFromTemplate(copy, 200, 200) });
      }
    } else if (editing.sourceId) {
      /* В§F-SLIB-07: РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РµРіРѕ custom С€С‚Р°РјРїР°. */
      updateCustomStamp(editing.sourceId, {
        width: saved.width,
        height: saved.height,
        shapes: [...shapes],
        texts: [...texts],
        images: [...images],
      });
    }

    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
    setEditing(null);
  };

    if (!tpl.custom) return;
    if (!window.confirm(`РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ С€С‚Р°РјРї В«${tpl.name}В» РёР· Р±РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєРё?`)) return;
    removeCustomStamp(tpl.id);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  };

      <button
        type="button"
        className="tool-btn"
        onClick={openCreate}
        style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}

      </button>

              type="button"
              className="tool-tile"

/* ============================================================
   presentation/components/stamps/CompositeFillLayer.tsx
   РћС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶Р°РµС‚ СЂР°СЃС‚СЂ anchor-Р·Р°Р»РёРІРѕРє. Р›РѕРіРёРєР° вЂ” РІ FillRasterizer.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '../../../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import { useFillRasterizer } from '../../hooks/useFillRasterizer';

interface Props {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  enabled?: boolean;
}

function anchorToWorld(
  target: StampShapeItem,
  local: { x: number; y: number },
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const lx = target.x + local.x * target.width;
  const ly = target.y + local.y * target.height;
  const cx = target.x + target.width / 2;
  const cy = target.y + target.height / 2;
  const rot = ((target.rotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dx = lx - cx;
  const dy = ly - cy;
  return {
    x: cx + dx * Math.cos(rot) - dy * Math.sin(rot),
    y: cy + dx * Math.sin(rot) + dy * Math.cos(rot),
  };
}

const CompositeFillLayerImpl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  width,
  height,
  shapes,
  enabled = true,
}) => {
  const fills: FillSpec[] = useMemo(() => {
    const out: FillSpec[] = [];
    for (const s of shapes) {
      if (s.kind !== 'fill') continue;
      if (s.hidden) continue;
      if (!s.fillTargetShapeId) continue;

      const target = shapes.find((t) => t.id === s.fillTargetShapeId);
      if (!target) continue;

      const local = s.fillAnchorLocal ?? { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 };
      const { x, y } = anchorToWorld(target, local);

      out.push({ px: x, py: y, color: s.fill || '#fbbf24', target });
    }
    return out;
  }, [shapes]);

  const signature = useMemo(() => {
    const geo = shapes
      .filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden)
      .map((s) =>
        `${s.kind}:${Math.round(s.x)}|${Math.round(s.y)}|${Math.round(s.width)}|${Math.round(s.height)}|${Math.round(s.rotation || 0)}|${(s.points || []).map((v) => Math.round(v)).join(',')}|${s.strokeWidth}`,
      )
      .join('||');
    const f = fills
      .map((x) => `${Math.round(x.px)},${Math.round(x.py)},${x.color},${x.target?.id ?? '-'}`)
      .join(';');
    return geo + '###' + f;
  }, [shapes, fills]);

  const entry = useFillRasterizer({
    key: signature,
    width,
    height,
    shapes,
    fills,
    enabled,
  });

  if (!enabled || !fills.length) return null;
  if (!entry || !entry.image) return null;

  return (
    <KImage
      x={0}
      y={0}
      width={width}
      height={height}
      image={entry.image}
      listening={false}
    />
  );
};

export const CompositeFillLayer = React.memo(CompositeFillLayerImpl);

import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { download } from '../../lib/download';
import { serializeSlideToSvg } from '../../lib/svg/serialize'; // <--- РќРћР’Р«Р™ РРњРџРћР Рў

export const Toolbar: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const data = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.data);

  const activeSlide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);

  const handleExportJson = () => {
    const payload = { version: '2.0', exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), slides, data };
    download(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`, 'application/json');
  };

  const handleExportSvg = () => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    const svgString = serializeSlideToSvg(activeSlide);
    download(svgString, `slide-${activeSlide.id}.svg`, 'image/svg+xml');
  };

  return (
    <header className="toolbar">
      <h1>Р РµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅРѕРІ</h1>
      <div className="toolbar-actions">
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={() => docStore.undo()} disabled={!docStore.canUndo}>в†¶ РћС‚РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={() => docStore.redo()} disabled={!docStore.canRedo}>в†· РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚СЊ</button>
        <div style={{ width: 16 }} />
        
        {/* РќРћР’РђРЇ РљРќРћРџРљРђ SVG */}

        <button className="toolbar-btn primary" onClick={handleExportJson}>рџ“Ґ Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚ JSON</button>
      </div>
    </header>

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { Slide, AnchorSide, ConnectorElement, CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import {
  findNearestAnchor, computeGhostRect, cloneForConnector,
  oppositeAnchor, type Rect
} from '../../../lib/connectors';
import { generateId } from '../../../shared/utils/id';

const ANCHOR_HIT_RADIUS = 30;

interface Draft {
  fromId: string;
  fromSide: AnchorSide;
  toX: number;
  toY: number;
}

export function useCanvasConnectorDraft(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  slide: Slide;
}) {
  const { docStore, slide } = params;
  const [draft, setDraftState] = useState<Draft | null>(null);
  const [ghostRect, setGhostRect] = useState<Rect | null>(null);
  const draftRef = useRef<Draft | null>(null);

  const setDraft = useCallback((d: Draft | null) => {
    draftRef.current = d;
    setDraftState(d);
    if (!d) setGhostRect(null);
  }, []);

  const startDraft = useCallback((fromId: string, fromSide: AnchorSide, wx: number, wy: number) => {
    setDraft({ fromId, fromSide, toX: wx, toY: wy });
    const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === fromId);
    if (source) {
      const rect = computeGhostRect(source, fromSide, wx, wy);
      setGhostRect(rect);
    }
  }, [setDraft, slide]);

  const update = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const d = draftRef.current;
    if (!d) return;

    const targetEl = slide.elements.find(
      (el) => el.id !== d.fromId && el.type !== 'connector' && !el.locked &&
      wx >= el.x && wx <= el.x + el.width && wy >= el.y && wy <= el.y + el.height
    );

    if (!targetEl) {
      const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === d.fromId);
      if (source) {
        const rect = computeGhostRect(source, d.fromSide, wx, wy);
        setGhostRect(rect);
      }
    } else {
      setGhostRect(null);
    }

    setDraft({ ...d, toX: wx, toY: wy });
  }, [slide]);

  const commit = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const d = draftRef.current;
    if (!d) return;

    const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === d.fromId);
    if (!source) {
      setDraft(null);
      return;
    }

    const nearest = slide.elements
      .filter((el) => el.id !== d.fromId && el.type !== 'connector' && !el.locked)
      .map((el) => ({ el, anchor: findNearestAnchor(el, wx, wy) }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.anchor.distance - b.anchor.distance)[0];

    docStore.commit((draftState) => {
      const s = draftState.slides.find((x) => x.id === draftState.activeId);
      if (!s) return;

      let toId = '';
      let toAnchor: AnchorSide = 'left';

      if (nearest && nearest.anchor.distance <= ANCHOR_HIT_RADIUS) {
        toId = nearest.el.id;
        toAnchor = nearest.anchor.side;
      } else if (ghostRect) {
        const newEl = cloneForConnector(ghostRect, source);
        newEl.id = generateId();
        toId = newEl.id;
        toAnchor = oppositeAnchor(d.fromSide);
        s.elements.push(newEl as CanvasElement);
      } else {
        setDraft(null);
        return;
      }

      const conn: ConnectorElement = {
        id: generateId(), type: 'connector',
        x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0, rotation: 0,
        fromId: d.fromId, fromAnchor: d.fromSide,
        toId: toId, toAnchor: toAnchor,
        stroke: '#374151', strokeWidth: 2, dash: 'solid',
        isArrow: true, routing: 'orthogonal',
      };
      s.elements.push(conn);
    });
    setDraft(null);
  }, [docStore, slide, ghostRect, setDraft]);

  const cancel = useCallback(() => setDraft(null), [setDraft]);

  return {
    startDraft, update, commit, cancel,
    current: () => draft,
    isDrafting: () => draftRef.current !== null,
    getGhostRect: () => ghostRect,
  };
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasDblClick.ts

/* ============================================================
   useCanvasDblClick.ts

   РџСЂРёРЅРёРјР°РµС‚ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ + viewport-РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚С‹ РѕС‚ Canvas.
   РќРµ Р·РЅР°РµС‚, РєР°РєРёРј СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµРј СЌС‚Рѕ Р±С‹Р»Рѕ РІС‹Р·РІР°РЅРѕ (Konva / DOM).
   ARC-PATTERN-08: С‡РёСЃС‚Р°СЏ Р»РѕРіРёРєР°, Р±РµР· РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРѕРє.
   ============================================================ */

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { findTableCellAt } from '../../../domain/slide/hitTest';

export interface DblClickViewport {
  viewportLeft: number;
  viewportTop: number;
  clientX: number;
  clientY: number;
}

export function useCanvasDblClick(params: {
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  zoom: number;
}) {
  const { modalsStore, zoom } = params;

  const handle = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, vp: DblClickViewport) => {
    /* Viewport-РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚С‹ (РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ: fixed РІ InlineEditor) */
    const screenX = vp.viewportLeft + el.x * zoom;
    const screenY = vp.viewportTop + el.y * zoom;
    const screenW = el.width * zoom;
    const screenH = el.height * zoom;

    /* ---------- text / footer: rich-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'text' || el.type === 'footer') {
      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id,
        field: el.type === 'footer' ? 'footerText' : 'text',
        kind: el.type,
        value: el.text ?? '',
        html: (el as any).html,
        x: screenX, y: screenY,
        width: screenW, height: screenH,
        fontSize: (el as any).fontSize ?? 16,
        fontFamily: (el as any).fontFamily ?? 'Arial',
        fontStyle: (el as any).fontStyle ?? 'normal',
        textDecoration: (el as any).textDecoration ?? '',
        fill: (el as any).fill ?? '#000',
        align: (el as any).align ?? 'left',
        verticalAlign: (el as any).verticalAlign ?? 'top',
        lineHeight: (el as any).lineHeight ?? 1.5,
        padding: (el as any).padding ?? { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        background: (el as any).background ?? 'transparent',
        isRich: el.type === 'text',
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- shape: inline plain-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'shape') {
      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id, field: 'shapeText', kind: 'shape',
        value: el.text ?? '',
        x: screenX, y: screenY,
        width: screenW, height: screenH,
        fontSize: el.fontSize ?? 16,
        fontFamily: el.textFontFamily ?? 'Arial',
        fontStyle: el.textFontStyle ?? 'normal',
        textDecoration: el.textDecoration ?? '',
        fill: el.textFill ?? '#000',
        align: el.textAlign ?? 'center',
        verticalAlign: el.textVerticalAlign ?? 'middle',
        lineHeight: el.textLineHeight ?? 1.3,
        padding: el.textPadding ?? { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 },
        background: 'transparent',
        isRich: false,
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- table: inline plain-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ РєРѕРЅРєСЂРµС‚РЅРѕР№ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'table') {
      const worldX = (vp.clientX - vp.viewportLeft) / zoom;
      const worldY = (vp.clientY - vp.viewportTop) / zoom;
      const cell = findTableCellAt(el, worldX, worldY);
      if (!cell) return;
      const cellW = (el.width * zoom) / el.cols;
      const cellH = (el.height * zoom) / el.rows;
      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id, field: 'tableCell', kind: 'table',
        tableRow: cell.row, tableCol: cell.col,
        value: el.data[cell.row]?.[cell.col] ?? '',
        x: screenX + cell.col * cellW,
        y: screenY + cell.row * cellH,
        width: cellW, height: cellH,
        fontSize: el.fontSize,
        fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
        fill: '#111', align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
        padding: { top: 6, right: 6, bottom: 6, left: 6 },
        background: '#fff', isRich: false,
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- stamp / diagram: РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РјРѕРґР°Р»РєРё ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'stamp') {
      modalsStore.openStampEditor(el.id);
      return;
    }
    if (el.type === 'diagram') {
      modalsStore.openDiagramEditor(el.id);
    }
  }, [modalsStore, zoom]);

  return { handle };
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasDrag.ts
SIZE: 3655 bytes

import { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { computeSnap } from '../../../services/snap';
import { guidesStore } from '../../stores/GuidesStore';

interface DragState {
  id: string;
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
  orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>;
  rafId: number | null;
}

export function useCanvasDrag(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  selStore: SelectionStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  slide: Slide;
}) {
  const { docStore, selStore, modalsStore, slide } = params;
  const state = useRef<DragState | null>(null);

  const onDragStart = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, e: any) => {
    modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);
    const selectedIds = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
    const ids = selectedIds.includes(el.id) ? selectedIds : [el.id];
    if (!selectedIds.includes(el.id)) selStore.setIds([el.id]);
    
    const orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
    for (const ee of slide.elements) {
      if (ids.includes(ee.id)) orig[ee.id] = { x: ee.x, y: ee.y };
    }
    state.current = { id: el.id, startX: e.target.x(), startY: e.target.y(), orig, rafId: null };
  }, [slide, selStore, modalsStore]);

  const onDragMove = useCallback((e: any) => {
    const s = state.current;
    if (!s || s.rafId !== null) return;
    
    s.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      const curr = state.current;
      if (!curr) return;
      curr.rafId = null;

      const x = e.target.x();
      const y = e.target.y();
      
      const el = slide.elements.find((el) => el.id === curr.id);
      
      if (el && !e.evt?.ctrlKey && !e.evt?.metaKey) {
        const snap = computeSnap(el, x, y, slide);
        const uniqueV = Array.from(new Set(snap.v));
        const uniqueH = Array.from(new Set(snap.h));
        guidesStore.set(uniqueV, uniqueH); 
      } else {
        guidesStore.clear();
      }

      const dx = x - curr.startX;
      const dy = y - curr.startY;
      
      const layer = e.target.getLayer();
      if (!layer) return;
      
      for (const [id, o] of Object.entries(curr.orig)) {
        if (id === curr.id) continue;
        const node = layer.findOne('#' + id);
        if (node) { node.x(o.x + dx); node.y(o.y + dy); }
      }
      layer.batchDraw();
    });
  }, [slide]);

  const onDragEnd = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, e: any) => {
    const s = state.current;
    if (!s) return;
    if (s.rafId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(s.rafId);
    state.current = null;
    guidesStore.clear();

    let endX = e.target.x();
    let endY = e.target.y();

    if (isNaN(endX) || isNaN(endY)) return;
    
    if (!e.evt?.ctrlKey && !e.evt?.metaKey) {
      const snap = computeSnap(el, endX, endY, slide);
      endX = snap.x;
      endY = snap.y;
    }

    const dx = endX - s.startX;
    const dy = endY - s.startY;
    const orig = s.orig;

    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const sl = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!sl) return;
      for (const ee of sl.elements) {
        const o = orig[ee.id];
        if (!o) continue;
        ee.x = ee.id === s.id ? endX : o.x + dx;
        ee.y = ee.id === s.id ? endY : o.y + dy;
      }
    }, 'move-group');
  }, [docStore, slide]);

  return { onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd };
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasDrop.ts
SIZE: 2420 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { RefObject } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import { makeArrayTable, makePlaceholder } from '../../../lib/factories';
import { resolveSample } from '../../../lib/resolveSample';
import { newStampFromTemplate } from '../../../lib/stamps/factories';
import { getAllStamps } from '../../../lib/stamps/library';

export function useCanvasDrop(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  stageRef: RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
  zoom: number;
}) {
  const { docStore, stageRef, zoom } = params;

  const onDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
  }, []);

  const onDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const raw = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/json');
    if (!raw) return;
    let payload: any;
    try { payload = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return; }

    const rect = stage.container().getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) / zoom;
    const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) / zoom;

    if (payload.kind === 'array') {
      const tbl = makeArrayTable(x, y, payload);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(tbl);
      }, 'drop-array');
      return;
    }
    if (payload.kind === 'field') {
      const data = docStore.getState().data;
      const sample = resolveSample(data, payload.path) ?? payload.sample ?? '';
      const ph = makePlaceholder(x, y, payload.path, payload.fieldType, sample);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(ph);
      }, 'drop-field');
      return;
    }
    if (payload.kind === 'stamp-template') {
      const tpl = getAllStamps().find((t) => t.id === payload.templateId);
      if (!tpl) return;
      const stamp = newStampFromTemplate(tpl, x, y);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(stamp);
      }, 'drop-stamp');
    }
  }, [docStore, stageRef, zoom]);

  return { onDragOver, onDrop };
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasFillMode.ts
SIZE: 2824 bytes

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement, FillElement, ShapeElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../../shared/utils/id';

export function useCanvasFillMode(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  slide: Slide;
}) {
  const { docStore, modalsStore, slide } = params;

  const applyFill = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const state = modalsStore.getState();
    const color = state.fillColor;

    // РС‰РµРј РІРµСЂС…РЅРёР№ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ РїРѕРґ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂРѕРј (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ shape РёР»Рё fill)
    const hit = slide.elements.slice().reverse().find((el) => {
      if (el.type !== 'shape' && el.type !== 'fill') return false;
      return wx >= el.x && wx <= el.x + el.width && wy >= el.y && wy <= el.y + el.height;
    });

    if (hit) {
      // F-FM-03: РљР»РёРє РїРѕ shape/fill вЂ” Р·Р°Р»РёРІР°РµРј Рё РћРЎРўРђР•РњРЎРЇ РІ СЂРµР¶РёРјРµ
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        const t = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === hit.id);
        if (!t) return;
        if (t.type === 'shape' || t.type === 'fill') {
          (t as ShapeElement | FillElement).fill = color;
        }
      });
      return;
    }

    // F-FM-04: РљР»РёРє РїРѕ РїСѓСЃС‚РѕРјСѓ РјРµСЃС‚Сѓ вЂ” РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([...state.fillPoints, wx, wy]);
  }, [docStore, modalsStore, slide]);

  const closePolygon = useCallback(() => {
    const state = modalsStore.getState();
    const pts = state.fillPoints;
    if (pts.length < 6) return;

    const xs: number[] = [];
    const ys: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) { xs.push(pts[i]); ys.push(pts[i + 1]); }
    const minX = Math.min(...xs);
    const minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
    const maxY = Math.max(...ys);

    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      const el: FillElement = {
        id: generateId(), type: 'fill',
        x: minX, y: minY,
        width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
        height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
        rotation: 0,
        points: pts.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY)),
        fill: state.fillColor, stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, opacity: 1,
      };
      s.elements.push(el as CanvasElement);
    });
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
  }, [docStore, modalsStore]);

  return {
    applyFill, closePolygon,
    points: () => modalsStore.getState().fillPoints,
  };
}

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../../shared/types';
import { findElementWithPadding } from '../../../lib/connectors';

export function useCanvasHoverAnchor(params: { slide: Slide }) {
  const { slide } = params;
  const [hoveredId, setHoveredId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const hoveredRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const update = useCallback((e: any, wx: number, wy: number) => {
    // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї 20px, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ С‚РѕС‡РєРё РЅРµ РїСЂРѕРїР°РґР°Р»Рё, РєРѕРіРґР° РјС‹ РІРµРґРµРј Рє РЅРёРј РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂ
    const el = findElementWithPadding(slide.elements, wx, wy, 20);
    
    if (el) {
      if (hoveredRef.current !== el.id) {
        hoveredRef.current = el.id;
        setHoveredId(el.id);
      }
    } else {
      if (hoveredRef.current !== null) {
        hoveredRef.current = null;
        setHoveredId(null);
      }
    }
  }, [slide]);

  return { update, getHoveredId: () => hoveredId };
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasRubberBand.ts
SIZE: 1630 bytes

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';

interface Box { x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number }

export function useCanvasRubberBand(params: { selStore: SelectionStore }) {
  const { selStore } = params;
  const [box, setBox] = useState<Box | null>(null);
  const active = useRef<Box | null>(null);

  const begin = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    active.current = { x1: x, y1: y, x2: x, y2: y };
    setBox(active.current);
  }, []);

  const update = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!active.current) return;
    active.current = {
      x1: Math.min(active.current.x1, x),
      y1: Math.min(active.current.y1, y),
      x2: x, y2: y,
    };
    setBox(active.current);
  }, []);

  const finish = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    if (!active.current) return;
    const b = active.current;
    active.current = null;
    setBox(null);
    if (Math.abs(b.x2 - b.x1) < 4 && Math.abs(b.y2 - b.y1) < 4) return;
    const ids = elements
      .filter((el) => {
        if (el.type === 'connector' || el.type === 'fill' || el.locked) return false;
        if (el.x + el.width < b.x1 || el.x > b.x2) return false;
        if (el.y + el.height < b.y1 || el.y > b.y2) return false;
        return true;
      })
      .map((el) => el.id);
    selStore.setIds(ids);
  }, [selStore]);

  return {
    begin, update, finish,
    isActive: () => active.current !== null,
    ref: () => box,
  };
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasViewport.ts
SIZE: 3297 bytes

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { RefObject } from 'react';

interface Args {
  zoom: number;
  onZoomChange: (z: number) => void;
  stageRef: RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
}

export function useCanvasViewport({ zoom, onZoomChange, stageRef }: Args) {
  const [isPanning, setIsPanning] = useState(false);
  const panStart = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0, scrollLeft: 0, scrollTop: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (e.code === 'Space' && !e.repeat && target.tagName !== 'INPUT' && target.tagName !== 'TEXTAREA') {
        setIsPanning(true);
      }
    };
    const onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.code === 'Space') setIsPanning(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleWheel = useCallback((e: any) => {
    if (!e.evt.ctrlKey && !e.evt.metaKey) return;
    e.evt.preventDefault();

    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const container = stage.container().parentElement?.parentElement; 
    if (!container) return;

    const oldZoom = zoom;
    const direction = e.evt.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1;
    
    // РЈР’Р•Р›РР§РР›Р Р“Р›РЈР‘РРќРЈ Р—РЈРњРђ: РѕС‚ 10% РґРѕ 400%
    const newZoom = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(4, oldZoom * (direction > 0 ? 1.1 : 1 / 1.1)));

    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mouseX = e.evt.clientX - rect.left;
    const mouseY = e.evt.clientY - rect.top;

    const docX = (mouseX + container.scrollLeft) / oldZoom;
    const docY = (mouseY + container.scrollTop) / oldZoom;

    onZoomChange(newZoom);

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      if (container) {
        container.scrollLeft = (docX * newZoom) - mouseX;
        container.scrollTop = (docY * newZoom) - mouseY;
      }
    });
  }, [zoom, onZoomChange, stageRef]);

  const handleMouseDown = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent | any) => {
    if (!isPanning) return;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const container = stage.container().parentElement?.parentElement;
    if (!container) return;

    panStart.current = {
      x: e.clientX,
      y: e.clientY,
      scrollLeft: container.scrollLeft,
      scrollTop: container.scrollTop,
    };

    const handleMouseMove = (moveEvent: MouseEvent) => {
      const dx = moveEvent.clientX - panStart.current.x;
      const dy = moveEvent.clientY - panStart.current.y;
      container.scrollLeft = panStart.current.scrollLeft - dx;
      container.scrollTop = panStart.current.scrollTop - dy;
    };

    const handleMouseUp = () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
    };

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
  }, [isPanning, stageRef]);

  return {
    isPanning,
    handleWheel,
    handleMouseDown,
  };
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\editor\useClipboardActions.ts
SIZE: 2104 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../../shared/utils/id';

export function useClipboardActions(
  docStore: DocumentStore,
  selStore: SelectionStore,
  modalsStore: ModalsStore,
) {
  const copy = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    const copyable = elements.filter((e) => e.type !== 'connector');
    if (!copyable.length) return;
    modalsStore.copy(copyable);
  }, [modalsStore]);

  const cut = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    const ids = elements.map((e) => e.id);
    if (!ids.length) return;
    copy(elements);
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => {
        if (ids.includes(el.id)) return false;
        if (el.type === 'connector' && (ids.includes(el.fromId) || ids.includes(el.toId))) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }, 'cut');
    selStore.clear();
  }, [docStore, selStore, copy]);

  const paste = useCallback(() => {
    const buf = modalsStore.getClipboard();
    if (!buf.length) return;
    const newIds: string[] = [];
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      for (const el of buf) {
        const clone = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
        clone.id = generateId();
        clone.x += 20;
        clone.y += 20;
        s.elements.push(clone);
        newIds.push(clone.id);
      }
    }, 'paste');
    selStore.setIds(newIds);
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);

  const duplicate = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    copy(elements);
    paste();
  }, [copy, paste]);

  return { copy, cut, paste, duplicate };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { DiagramElement } from '../../../shared/types';

export function useDiagramCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((elementId: string, patch: Partial<DiagramElement>) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((e) => e.id === elementId);
      if (!el || el.type !== 'diagram') return;
      if (patch.svg !== undefined) el.svg = patch.svg;
      if (patch.xml !== undefined) el.xml = patch.xml;
    }, `diagram-${elementId}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\editor\useEditingCommit.ts

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { EditingState } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';

export function useEditingCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((editing: EditingState, html: string | undefined, text: string) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const slide = draft.slides.find((s) => s.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = slide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === editing.id);
      if (!el) return;

      const safeText = text ?? '';

      switch (editing.field) {
        case 'text': {
          if (el.type !== 'text') return;
          // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј, РµСЃС‚СЊ Р»Рё РІ HTML С‡С‚Рѕ-С‚Рѕ, РєСЂРѕРјРµ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹С… РїР°СЂР°РіСЂР°С„РѕРІ (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р°)
          const hasRichContent = html && (html.includes('<table') || html.includes('<img'));
          const isEmpty = !safeText.trim() && !hasRichContent;
          
          el.text = isEmpty ? 'Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ С‚РµРєСЃС‚' : safeText;
          el.html = isEmpty ? undefined : html;
          return;
        }
        case 'footerText':
          if (el.type !== 'footer') return;
          el.text = safeText;
          if (html !== undefined) el.html = html || undefined;
          return;
        case 'shapeText':
          if (el.type !== 'shape') return;
          el.text = safeText;
          return;
        case 'tableCell': {
          if (el.type !== 'table') return;
          const r = editing.tableRow ?? 0;
          const c = editing.tableCol ?? 0;
          const rows = el.data.map((row) => [...row]);
          if (rows[r] && c < rows[r].length) rows[r][c] = safeText;
          el.data = rows;
          return;
        }
        default:
          return;
      }
    }, `commit-${editing.id}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { StampElement } from '../../../shared/types';

export function useStampCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((elementId: string, patch: Partial<StampElement>) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((e) => e.id === elementId);
      if (!el || el.type !== 'stamp') return;
      if (patch.width !== undefined) el.width = patch.width;
      if (patch.height !== undefined) el.height = patch.height;
      if (patch.shapes !== undefined) el.shapes = patch.shapes.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
      if (patch.texts !== undefined) el.texts = patch.texts.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
      if (patch.images !== undefined) el.images = patch.images.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
    }, `stamp-${elementId}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';

export function useStyleClipboard(
  docStore: DocumentStore,
  selStore: SelectionStore,
  modalsStore: ModalsStore,
) {
  const copyStyle = useCallback((el: CanvasElement) => {
    modalsStore.copyStyle(el);
  }, [modalsStore]);

  const applyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    const style = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard();
    const id = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
    if (!style || !id) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (el) Object.assign(el, style);
    }, `style-${id}`);
    modalsStore.clearStyle();
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);

  return { copyStyle, applyStyle };
}

/**
 * Anchor Overlay Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle anchor points for connector creation
 */

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';

export interface AnchorPoint {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  position: 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
  elementId: string;
}

interface UseAnchorOverlayReturn {
  hoveredElementId: string | null;
  anchors: AnchorPoint[];
  showAnchors: (elementId: string, anchors: AnchorPoint[]) => void;
  hideAnchors: () => void;
  setHoveredElement: (id: string | null) => void;
}

export function useAnchorOverlay(): UseAnchorOverlayReturn {
  const [hoveredElementId, setHoveredElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [anchors, setAnchors] = useState<AnchorPoint[]>([]);

  const showAnchors = useCallback((elementId: string, newAnchors: AnchorPoint[]) => {
    setHoveredElementId(elementId);
    setAnchors(newAnchors);
  }, []);

  const hideAnchors = useCallback(() => {
    setHoveredElementId(null);
    setAnchors([]);
  }, []);

  const setHoveredElement = useCallback((id: string | null) => {
    setHoveredElementId(id);
    if (!id) {
      setAnchors([]);
    }
  }, []);

  return {
    hoveredElementId,
    anchors,
    showAnchors,
    hideAnchors,
    setHoveredElement,
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useAutoFitZoom.ts
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface Args {
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  slideWidth: number;
  slideHeight: number;
  setZoom: (z: number) => void;
  padding?: number;
  topReserve?: number;
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
}

export function useAutoFitZoom({
  wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom,
  padding = 40, topReserve = 80, minZoom = 0.2, maxZoom = 2,
}: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    if (!wrap || !slideWidth || !slideHeight) return;

    const fit = () => {
      const rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
      // РћС‚РЅРёРјР°РµРј РїР°РґРґРёРЅРіРё Рё СЂРµР·РµСЂРІ РїРѕРґ ZoomBar
      const availableW = Math.max(100, rect.width - padding * 2);
      const availableH = Math.max(100, rect.height - padding * 2 - topReserve);

      const zoomX = availableW / slideWidth;
      const zoomY = availableH / slideHeight;
      const next = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, Math.min(zoomX, zoomY)));

      setZoom(+next.toFixed(3));
    };

    fit();

    let raf = 0;
    const onResize = () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(fit);
    };

    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
    };
  }, [wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom, padding, topReserve, minZoom, maxZoom]);
}

/* ============================================================
   useAutoSave.ts
   F-AS-02: Debounce 2000ms.
   F-AS-03: TTL 24h.
   REG-B8: РќРµ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РІРІРѕРґ (Р°СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРЅР°СЏ Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ).
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Slide, DataRoot } from '../../shared/types';

const AUTO_SAVE_KEY = 'ticket-editor:autosave';
const AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS = 2000;
const MAX_AGE_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export interface AutoSaveData {
  slides: Slide[];
  data: DataRoot[];
  activeSlideId: string | null;
  savedAt: string;
}

export function useAutoSave(
  slides: Slide[],
  data: DataRoot[],
  activeSlideId: string | null,
): void {
  const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (timerRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    
    timerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      const saveData: AutoSaveData = {
        slides, data, activeSlideId,
        savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      
      // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј requestIdleCallback (РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ UI
      const doSave = () => {
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(saveData));
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('[useAutoSave] Failed to save:', err);
        }
      };

      if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
        (window as any).requestIdleCallback(doSave);
      } else {
        setTimeout(doSave, 0);
      }
    }, AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => {
      if (timerRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    };
  }, [slides, data, activeSlideId]);
}

export function loadAutoSave(): AutoSaveData | null {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY);
    if (!saved) return null;
    const data: AutoSaveData = JSON.parse(saved);
    
    if (new Date().getTime() - new Date(data.savedAt).getTime() > MAX_AGE_MS) {
      clearAutoSave();
      return null;
    }
    return data;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function clearAutoSave(): void {
  try { localStorage.removeItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY); } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

// src/presentation/hooks/useConnectors.ts

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement, ConnectorElement, AnchorSide } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

export interface ConnectorDraft {
  fromId: string;
  fromAnchor: AnchorSide;
  toX: number;
  toY: number;
  hoverTargetId?: string;
  hoverAnchor?: AnchorSide;
}

interface UseConnectorsOptions {
  slide: Slide | null;
  addElement: (element: CanvasElement) => void;
  removeElement: (id: string) => void;
}

export interface UseConnectorsReturn {
  draft: ConnectorDraft | null;
  startDraft: (fromId: string, fromAnchor: AnchorSide, x: number, y: number) => void;
  moveDraft: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  commitDraft: () => void;
  cancelDraft: () => void;
  findNearestAnchor: (x: number, y: number) => { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide } | null;
}

const ANCHOR_OFFSET = 8;

export function useConnectors(options: UseConnectorsOptions): UseConnectorsReturn {
  const { slide, addElement } = options;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ConnectorDraft | null>(null);

  const getAnchorPosition = useCallback((
    element: CanvasElement,
    anchor: AnchorSide
  ): { x: number; y: number } => {
    const cx = element.x + element.width / 2;
    const cy = element.y + element.height / 2;

    switch (anchor) {
      case 'top': return { x: cx, y: element.y - ANCHOR_OFFSET };
      case 'right': return { x: element.x + element.width + ANCHOR_OFFSET, y: cy };
      case 'bottom': return { x: cx, y: element.y + element.height + ANCHOR_OFFSET };
      case 'left': return { x: element.x - ANCHOR_OFFSET, y: cy };
    }
  }, []);

  const findNearestAnchor = useCallback((
    x: number,
    y: number
  ): { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide } | null => {
    if (!slide) return null;
    let nearest: { elementId: string; anchor: AnchorSide; dist: number } | null = null;
    for (const el of slide.elements) {
      if (el.type === 'connector' || el.type === 'fill') continue;
      const anchors: AnchorSide[] = ['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left'];
      for (const anchor of anchors) {
        const pos = getAnchorPosition(el, anchor);
        const dist = Math.hypot(pos.x - x, pos.y - y);
        if (dist < 30 && (!nearest || dist < nearest.dist)) {
          nearest = { elementId: el.id, anchor, dist };
        }
      }
    }
    return nearest ? { elementId: nearest.elementId, anchor: nearest.anchor } : null;
  }, [slide, getAnchorPosition]);

  const startDraft = useCallback((fromId: string, fromAnchor: AnchorSide, x: number, y: number) => {
    setDraft({ fromId, fromAnchor, toX: x, toY: y });
  }, []);

  const moveDraft = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!draft) return;
    const nearest = findNearestAnchor(x, y);
    setDraft({ ...draft, toX: x, toY: y, hoverTargetId: nearest?.elementId, hoverAnchor: nearest?.anchor });
  }, [draft, findNearestAnchor]);

  const commitDraft = useCallback(() => {
    if (!draft || !slide) return;
    if (!draft.hoverTargetId || !draft.hoverAnchor || draft.fromId === draft.hoverTargetId) {
      setDraft(null);
      return;
    }
    const connector: ConnectorElement = {
      id: generateId(), type: 'connector', x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0, rotation: 0,
      fromId: draft.fromId, fromAnchor: draft.fromAnchor,
      toId: draft.hoverTargetId, toAnchor: draft.hoverAnchor,
      stroke: '#374151', strokeWidth: 2, dash: 'solid', isArrow: true, routing: 'straight',
    };
    addElement(connector);
    setDraft(null);
  }, [draft, slide, addElement]);

  const cancelDraft = useCallback(() => setDraft(null), []);

  return { draft, startDraft, moveDraft, commitDraft, cancelDraft, findNearestAnchor };
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\useEditorShortcuts.ts
