import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore, DocumentState } from '../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

interface Args {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  selStore: SelectionStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
}

const isEditable = (el: EventTarget | null): boolean => {
  const h = el as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!h) return false;
  return (
    h.isContentEditable ||
    h.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
    h.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
    h.tagName === 'SELECT'
  );
};

/**
 * Р“Р»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ С€РѕСЂС‚РєР°С‚С‹ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР° (В§38).
 *
 * - РќРµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ РїСЂРё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹С… РјРѕРґР°Р»РєР°С… (В§F-STMP-E-17, В§F-DG вЂ” СЃР°РјРё РѕР±СЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°СЋС‚).
 * - РќРµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚, РєРѕРіРґР° С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР° вЂ” РєСЂРѕРјРµ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°СЏ, РєРѕРіРґР° РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚
 *   inline-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ (С‚РѕРіРґР° Escape РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РµРіРѕ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ вЂ” В§F-IE-07).
 * - MergeKey РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ continuous-update (`update-<id>`), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹
 *   РЅРµ СЃРєР»РµРёС‚СЊ РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ РёРґСѓС‰РёРµ delete/paste/duplicate РІ РѕРґРЅСѓ Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ (В§I-13, В§I-14).
 */
export function useEditorShortcuts({ docStore, selStore, modalsStore }: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const ms = modalsStore.getState();

      /* РњРѕРґР°Р»РєРё (StampEditor, DiagramEditor) СЃР°РјРё РІРµС€Р°СЋС‚ СЃРІРѕРё РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‡РёРєРё. */
      if (ms.stampEditorTarget || ms.diagramEditorTarget) return;

      /* Р¤РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР° Рё inline-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ РЅРµ Р°РєС‚РёРІРµРЅ вЂ” РЅРµ РјРµС€Р°РµРј. */
      if (isEditable(e.target) && !ms.editing) return;

      const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
      const code = e.code;

      /* -------- Escape (В§F-IE-07, В§F-FM-06) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (ms.editing) {
          modalsStore.closeEditor();
          return;
        }
        if (ms.fillPoints.length) {
          modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
          return;
        }
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Enter: Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РїРѕР»РёРіРѕРЅ fill (В§F-FM-05) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !ms.editing && ms.fillPoints.length >= 6) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const pts = ms.fillPoints.slice();
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          const xs: number[] = [];
          const ys: number[] = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) {
            xs.push(pts[i]);
            ys.push(pts[i + 1]);
          }
          const minX = Math.min(...xs);
          const minY = Math.min(...ys);
          const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
          const maxY = Math.max(...ys);
          const fillEl: CanvasElement = {
            id: generateId(),
            type: 'fill',
            x: minX,
            y: minY,
            width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
            height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
            rotation: 0,
            points: pts.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY)),
            fill: ms.fillColor,
            stroke: '',
            strokeWidth: 0,
            opacity: 1,
          } as CanvasElement;
          s.elements.push(fillEl);
        });
        modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
        return;
      }

      /* Р”Р°Р»СЊС€Рµ вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРіРґР° С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РЅРµ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР°. */
      if (isEditable(e.target)) return;

      /* -------- Delete / Backspace (В§38) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        if (!ids.length) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => !ids.includes(el.id));
        });
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Undo / Redo (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyZ' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.undo();
        return;
      }
      if (ctrl && (code === 'KeyY' || (code === 'KeyZ' && e.shiftKey))) {
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.redo();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Copy / Cut / Paste / Duplicate (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyC') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (els.length) modalsStore.copy(els);
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyX') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (!els.length) return;
        modalsStore.copy(els);
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => !ids.includes(el.id));
        });
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyV') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const buf = modalsStore.getClipboard();
        if (!buf.length) return;
        const newIds: string[] = [];
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          for (const el of buf) {
            const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
            cloned.id = generateId();
            (cloned as any).x += 20;
            (cloned as any).y += 20;
            s.elements.push(cloned);
            newIds.push(cloned.id);
          }
        });
        selStore.setIds(newIds);
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyD') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (!els.length) return;
        const newIds: string[] = [];
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          for (const el of els) {
            const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
            cloned.id = generateId();
            (cloned as any).x += 20;
            (cloned as any).y += 20;
            s.elements.push(cloned);
            newIds.push(cloned.id);
          }
        });
        selStore.setIds(newIds);
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Copy / Apply style (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && e.altKey && code === 'KeyC') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const selId = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
        const el = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === selId);
        if (el) modalsStore.copyStyle(el);
        return;
      }
      if (ctrl && e.altKey && code === 'KeyV') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const style = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard();
        if (!style) return;
        const selId = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
        if (!selId) return;
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === selId);
          if (el) Object.assign(el, style);
        });
        modalsStore.clearStyle();
        return;
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);
}

// src/presentation/hooks/useFillMode.ts

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement, FillElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

interface UseFillModeOptions {
  slide: Slide | null;
  addElement: (element: CanvasElement) => void;
  updateElement: (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
}

export interface UseFillModeReturn {
  isActive: boolean;
  color: string;
  points: readonly number[];
  activate: () => void;
  deactivate: () => void;
  setColor: (color: string) => void;
  handleClick: (pointerPos: { x: number; y: number }) => boolean;
  closePolygon: () => void;
  cancel: () => void;
  reset: () => void;
}

function hitShapeElement(el: CanvasElement, px: number, py: number): boolean {
  if (el.type === 'shape') {
    if (px < el.x || py < el.y || px > el.x + el.width || py > el.y + el.height) return false;
    if (el.shape === 'circle') {
      const cx = el.x + el.width / 2, cy = el.y + el.height / 2;
      const r = Math.min(el.width, el.height) / 2;
      return Math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy) <= r;
    }
    return true;
  }
  if (el.type === 'fill' && el.points && el.points.length >= 6) {
    const pts = el.points;
    const n = pts.length / 2;
    let inside = false;
    for (let i = 0, j = n - 1; i < n; j = i++) {
      const xi = el.x + pts[i * 2], yi = el.y + pts[i * 2 + 1];
      const xj = el.x + pts[j * 2], yj = el.y + pts[j * 2 + 1];
      const intersect = yi > py !== yj > py && px < ((xj - xi) * (py - yi)) / (yj - yi + 0.000001) + xi;
      if (intersect) inside = !inside;
    }
    return inside;
  }
  return false;
}

function findShapeAt(slide: Slide | null, x: number, y: number): CanvasElement | null {
  if (!slide) return null;
  for (let i = slide.elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = slide.elements[i];
    if (hitShapeElement(el, x, y)) return el;
  }
  return null;
}

export function useFillMode(options: UseFillModeOptions): UseFillModeReturn {
  const { slide, addElement, updateElement } = options;
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(false);
  const [color, setColor] = useState('#fbbf24');
  const [points, setPoints] = useState<number[]>([]);

  const activate = useCallback(() => setIsActive(true), []);
  const deactivate = useCallback(() => { setIsActive(false); setPoints([]); }, []);
  const cancel = useCallback(() => setPoints([]), []);
  const reset = useCallback(() => { setIsActive(false); setPoints([]); setColor('#fbbf24'); }, []);

  const handleClick = useCallback((pointerPos: { x: number; y: number }): boolean => {
    if (!isActive) return false;
    const target = findShapeAt(slide, pointerPos.x, pointerPos.y);
    if (target && (target.type === 'shape' || target.type === 'fill')) {
      updateElement(target.id, { fill: color } as any);
      return true;
    }
    setPoints((prev) => [...prev, pointerPos.x, pointerPos.y]);
    return true;
  }, [isActive, color, slide, updateElement]);

  const closePolygon = useCallback(() => {
    if (points.length < 6) { setPoints([]); return; }
    const xs: number[] = [], ys: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i += 2) { xs.push(points[i]); ys.push(points[i + 1]); }
    const minX = Math.min(...xs), minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs), maxY = Math.max(...ys);
    const relativePoints = points.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY));
    const fillElement: FillElement = {
      id: generateId(), type: 'fill', x: minX, y: minY,
      width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX), height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
      rotation: 0, points: relativePoints, fill: color, stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, opacity: 1,
    };
    addElement(fillElement);
    setPoints([]);
  }, [points, color, addElement]);

  return { isActive, color, points, activate, deactivate, setColor, handleClick, closePolygon, cancel, reset };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useFillRasterizer.ts
   РўРѕРЅРєР°СЏ РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєР° РЅР°Рґ singleton FillRasterizer:
   РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРєР° РЅР° СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚ + Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РїСЂРё РјРѕРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё/СЃРјРµРЅРµ key.
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '../../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import {
  getFillRasterizer,
  type FillCacheEntry,
} from '../../lib/stamps/fillRasterizer';

export interface UseFillRasterizerArgs {
  key: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
  enabled: boolean;
}

export function useFillRasterizer(args: UseFillRasterizerArgs): FillCacheEntry | null {
  const { key, width, height, shapes, fills, enabled } = args;

  const [entry, setEntry] = useState<FillCacheEntry | null>(
    () => getFillRasterizer().get(key) ?? null,
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const rasterizer = getFillRasterizer();
    const cached = rasterizer.get(key);
    if (cached) setEntry(cached);
    return rasterizer.subscribe((changedKey, changedEntry) => {
      if (changedKey === key) setEntry(changedEntry);
    });
  }, [key]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const rasterizer = getFillRasterizer();
    if (rasterizer.get(key)) return;
    if (rasterizer.isInflight(key)) return;
    rasterizer.request({ key, width, height, shapes, fills });
  }, [key, width, height, shapes, fills, enabled]);

  return entry;
}

/**
 * Group Drag Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle dragging multiple selected elements
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';

interface DragState {
  isDragging: boolean;
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
}

interface UseGroupDragReturn {
  dragState: DragState;
  startDrag: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  updateDrag: (x: number, y: number) => { dx: number; dy: number } | null;
  endDrag: () => void;
}

export function useGroupDrag(): UseGroupDragReturn {
  const [dragState, setDragState] = useState<DragState>({
    isDragging: false,
    startX: 0,
    startY: 0,
  });
  
  const lastPosRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  const startDrag = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    setDragState({
      isDragging: true,
      startX: x,
      startY: y,
    });
    lastPosRef.current = { x, y };
  }, []);

  const updateDrag = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!dragState.isDragging) return null;
    
    const dx = x - lastPosRef.current.x;
    const dy = y - lastPosRef.current.y;
    
    lastPosRef.current = { x, y };
    
    return { dx, dy };
  }, [dragState.isDragging]);

  const endDrag = useCallback(() => {
    setDragState({
      isDragging: false,
      startX: 0,
      startY: 0,
    });
  }, []);

  return {
    dragState,
    startDrag,
    updateDrag,
    endDrag,
  };
}

// src/presentation/hooks/useHistory.ts

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { produce } from 'immer';
import type { Slide } from '../../shared/types';
import { MAX_HISTORY } from '../../shared/constants';

export interface HistoryEntry { slides: Slide[]; activeSlideId: string | null; }
export interface UseHistoryReturn {
  canUndo: boolean;
  canRedo: boolean;
  push: (slides: Slide[], activeSlideId: string | null) => void;
  undo: () => HistoryEntry | null;
  redo: () => HistoryEntry | null;
  reset: () => void;
}

export function useHistory(): UseHistoryReturn {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<HistoryEntry[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(-1);
  const lastCommittedRef = useRef<Slide[] | null>(null);

  const push = useCallback((slides: Slide[], activeSlideId: string | null) => {
    const last = lastCommittedRef.current;
    if (last === slides) return;
    const snapshot = last ? produce(last, (draft) => {
      if (draft.length !== slides.length) draft.length = slides.length;
      for (let i = 0; i < slides.length; i++) { if (draft[i] !== slides[i]) draft[i] = slides[i]; }
    }) : slides;
    lastCommittedRef.current = snapshot;
    setEntries((prev) => {
      const trimmed = prev.slice(0, index + 1);
      trimmed.push({ slides: snapshot, activeSlideId });
      while (trimmed.length > MAX_HISTORY) trimmed.shift();
      return trimmed;
    });
    setIndex((prev) => (prev + 1 >= MAX_HISTORY ? MAX_HISTORY - 1 : prev + 1));
  }, [index]);

  const undo = useCallback((): HistoryEntry | null => {
    if (index <= 0) return null;
    const prev = entries[index - 1];
    lastCommittedRef.current = prev.slides;
    setIndex(index - 1);
    return prev;
  }, [entries, index]);

  const redo = useCallback((): HistoryEntry | null => {
    if (index >= entries.length - 1) return null;
    const next = entries[index + 1];
    lastCommittedRef.current = next.slides;
    setIndex(index + 1);
    return next;
  }, [entries, index]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => { setEntries([]); setIndex(-1); lastCommittedRef.current = null; }, []);

  return { canUndo: index > 0, canRedo: index < entries.length - 1, push, undo, redo, reset };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide, DataRoot } from '../../shared/types';
import { CONFIG } from '../../shared/config';

export function useIO() {
  const exportJSON = useCallback((slides: Slide[], data: DataRoot[]) => {
    const exportData = { version: '2.0', slides, data };
    const json = JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2);
    const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    
    clearAutoSave(); // F-AS-06
  }, []);

  const importJSON = useCallback((file: File, onSuccess: (slides: Slide[], data: DataRoot[]) => void, onError: (error: string) => void) => {
    // NFR-SEC-03: Р›РёРјРёС‚ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂР°
    if (file.size > CONFIG.MAX_IMPORT_SIZE) {
      onError(`Р¤Р°Р№Р» СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј Р±РѕР»СЊС€РѕР№. РњР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј ${CONFIG.MAX_IMPORT_SIZE / 1024 / 1024} MB.`);
      return;
    }

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      try {
        const content = e.target?.result as string;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(content);
        
        if (!parsed.slides || !Array.isArray(parsed.slides)) {
          throw new Error('РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ С„Р°Р№Р»Р°: РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ СЃР»Р°Р№РґРѕРІ');
        }

        // NFR-SEC-03: Р›РёРјРёС‚С‹ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІР°
        if (parsed.slides.length > CONFIG.MAX_SLIDES) {
          throw new Error(`РЎР»РёС€РєРѕРј РјРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃР»Р°Р№РґРѕРІ. РњР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј ${CONFIG.MAX_SLIDES}.`);
        }
        for (const slide of parsed.slides) {
          if (slide.elements && slide.elements.length > CONFIG.MAX_ELEMENTS_PER_SLIDE) {
            throw new Error(`РЎР»РёС€РєРѕРј РјРЅРѕРіРѕ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ РЅР° СЃР»Р°Р№РґРµ. РњР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј ${CONFIG.MAX_ELEMENTS_PER_SLIDE}.`);
          }
        }

        const version = parsed.version || '1.0';
        const slides = migrateSlides(parsed.slides, version);
        
        onSuccess(slides, parsed.data || []);
      } catch (err) {
        onError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'РћС€РёР±РєР° С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ С„Р°Р№Р»Р°');
      }
    };
    reader.onerror = () => onError('РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РїСЂРѕС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р»');
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }, []);

  return { exportJSON, importJSON };
}

// MIG-DATA-01..06
function migrateSlides(slides: any[], version: string): Slide[] {
  let currentSlides = [...slides];
  let currentVersion = version;

  if (currentVersion === '1.0') {
    currentSlides = currentSlides.map((slide) => ({
      ...slide,
      elements: (slide.elements || []).map((el: any) => ({
        ...el,
        rotation: el.rotation || 0,
        html: el.type === 'text' && !el.html ? `<p>${el.text || ''}</p>` : el.html,
      })),
    }));
    currentVersion = '1.5';
  }

  if (currentVersion === '1.5') {
    currentSlides = currentSlides.map((slide) => ({
      ...slide,
      elements: (slide.elements || []).map((el: any) => ({
        ...el,
        type: el.type === 'rect' ? 'shape' : el.type,
        shape: el.type === 'rect' ? 'rect' : el.shape,
      })),
    }));
    currentVersion = '2.0';
  }

  return currentSlides as Slide[];
}

/**
 * Rubber Band (Lasso) Hook - Presentation Layer
 * Handle rectangular selection on canvas
 */

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';

interface Point { x: number; y: number }

interface RubberBandState {
  isActive: boolean;
  start: Point | null;
  current: Point | null;
}

interface UseRubberBandReturn {
  rubberBand: RubberBandState;
  handleMouseDown: (pos: Point) => void;
  handleMouseMove: (pos: Point) => void;
  handleMouseUp: () => { start: Point; end: Point } | null;
  reset: () => void;
}

export function useRubberBand(): UseRubberBandReturn {
  const [state, setState] = useState<RubberBandState>({
    isActive: false,
    start: null,
    current: null,
  });
  
  const startRef = useRef<Point | null>(null);

  const handleMouseDown = useCallback((pos: Point) => {
    startRef.current = pos;
    setState({
      isActive: true,
      start: pos,
      current: pos,
    });
  }, []);

  const handleMouseMove = useCallback((pos: Point) => {
    if (!state.isActive) return;
    setState(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      current: pos,
    }));
  }, [state.isActive]);

  const handleMouseUp = useCallback(() => {
    if (!state.isActive || !state.start || !state.current) {
      return null;
    }
    
    const result = {
      start: state.start,
      end: state.current,
    };
    
    setState({
      isActive: false,
      start: null,
      current: null,
    });
    startRef.current = null;
    
    return result;
  }, [state.isActive, state.start, state.current]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setState({
      isActive: false,
      start: null,
      current: null,
    });
    startRef.current = null;
  }, []);

  return {
    rubberBand: state,
    handleMouseDown,
    handleMouseMove,
    handleMouseUp,
    reset,
  };
}

import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';

export function useSelectedElement() {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();

  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);

  if (selectedIds.length !== 1) return null;
  const slide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);
  return slide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === selectedIds[0]) ?? null;
}

FILE: presentation\hooks\useSelection.ts

import { useState, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

export interface UseSelectionReturn {
  selectedIds: readonly string[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedElements: CanvasElement[];
  isSelected: (id: string) => boolean;
  select: (id: string, multi?: boolean) => void;
  selectMany: (ids: string[]) => void;
  clearSelection: () => void;
  toggleSelection: (id: string) => void;
}

export function useSelection(elements: CanvasElement[]): UseSelectionReturn {
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const selectedId = useMemo(() => selectedIds[0] ?? null, [selectedIds]);

  const selectedElements = useMemo(() => {
    return elements.filter((el) => selectedIds.includes(el.id));
  }, [elements, selectedIds]);

  const isSelected = useCallback(
    (id: string) => selectedIds.includes(id),
    [selectedIds],
  );

  const select = useCallback((id: string, multi = false) => {
    if (!id) {
      setSelectedIds([]);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedIds((prev) => {
      if (!multi) return [id];
      return prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id];
    });
  }, []);

  const selectMany = useCallback((ids: string[]) => {
    setSelectedIds(ids);
  }, []);

  const clearSelection = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedIds([]);
  }, []);

  const toggleSelection = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id],
    );
  }, []);

  return {
    selectedIds,
    selectedId,
    selectedElements,
    isSelected,
    select,
    selectMany,
    clearSelection,
    toggleSelection,
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useStampFillMode.ts

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';

export function useStampFillMode({

  texts,
  images,
  width,
  height,
  commit,
  select,
}: UseStampFillModeArgs): UseStampFillModeReturn {
  const [fillMode, setFillMode] = useState(false);
  const [fillColor, setFillColor] = useState('#fbbf24');

  const handleCanvasClick = useCallback((px: number, py: number) => {
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] enter', {
      fillMode,
      px, py,
      shapesCount: shapes.length,
      kinds: shapes.map((s) => `${s.kind}:${s.id.slice(0, 6)}`),
    });

    if (!fillMode) return;

    const containing = findShapesContaining(px, py, shapes);
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] containing', containing.length,
      containing.map((s) => `${s.kind}:${s.id.slice(0, 6)}`));

    if (containing.length === 0) {
      console.warn('[useStampFillMode] no shape at', { px, py,
        candidates: shapes
          .filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden)
          .map((s) => ({
            id: s.id.slice(0, 6), kind: s.kind,
            x: s.x, y: s.y, w: s.width, h: s.height,
          })),
      });
      return;
    }

    const target = containing.reduce((a, b) =>
      a.width * a.height <= b.width * b.height ? a : b,
    );
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] target', target.kind, target.id.slice(0, 6),
      { x: target.x, y: target.y, w: target.width, h: target.height });

    const localX = (px - target.x) / Math.max(1, target.width);
    const localY = (py - target.y) / Math.max(1, target.height);

    const fill: StampShapeItem = {
      id: generateId(),
      kind: 'fill',
      x: 0, y: 0,
      width, height,
      rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
      stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
      fill: fillColor,
      fillTargetShapeId: target.id,
      fillAnchorLocal: { x: localX, y: localY },
      name: 'Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё',
    };

    console.log('[useStampFillMode] committing fill', fill.id.slice(0, 6),
      'anchor', { x: localX, y: localY });

    commit([...shapes, fill], texts, images);
    select(fill.id, 'shape');
    setFillMode(false);
  }, [fillMode, fillColor, shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select]);

  return { fillMode, setFillMode, fillColor, setFillColor, handleCanvasClick };
}

import React, { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
import { DocumentStore } from '../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';

export interface EditorStores {
  document: DocumentStore;
  selection: SelectionStore;
  modals: ModalsStore;
}

const EditorContext = createContext<EditorStores | null>(null);

export const EditorProvider: React.FC<{ stores: EditorStores; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ stores, children }) => (
  <EditorContext.Provider value={stores}>{children}</EditorContext.Provider>
);

export function useStores(): EditorStores {
  const ctx = useContext(EditorContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useStores must be used within EditorProvider');
  return ctx;
}

export const useDocumentStore = () => useStores().document;
export const useSelectionStore = () => useStores().selection;
export const useModalsStore = () => useStores().modals;

/* ============================================================
   GuidesStore.ts
   РњРёРєСЂРѕ-СЃС‚РѕСЂ РґР»СЏ Р»РёРЅРёР№ РїСЂРёР»РёРїР°РЅРёСЏ.
   РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕСЂР° РќР• РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚ СЂРµСЂРµРЅРґРµСЂ Canvas,
   Р° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚ GuidesLayer.
   ============================================================ */

export class GuidesStore {
  private state = { v: [] as number[], h: [] as number[] };
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  subscribe = (listener: () => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  };

  getSnapshot = () => this.state;

  set = (v: number[], h: number[]) => {
    // РџСЂРµРґРѕС‚РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј Р»РёС€РЅРёРµ РІС‹Р·РѕРІС‹, РµСЃР»Рё Р»РёРЅРёРё РЅРµ РёР·РјРµРЅРёР»РёСЃСЊ
    if (this.state.v.length === v.length && this.state.h.length === h.length) {
      let same = true;
      for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) if (this.state.v[i] !== v[i]) { same = false; break; }
      if (same) for (let i = 0; i < h.length; i++) if (this.state.h[i] !== h[i]) { same = false; break; }
      if (same) return;
    }
    this.state = { v, h };
    this.listeners.forEach(l => l());
  };

  clear = () => this.set([], []);
}

export const guidesStore = new GuidesStore();

import type {

export interface EditingState {

export interface ContextMenuState {

}

export interface ModalsState {
  editing: EditingState | null;
  floatingPos: { x: number; y: number } | null;
  contextMenu: ContextMenuState | null;
  stampEditorTarget: string | null;
  diagramEditorTarget: string | null;
  fillMode: boolean;
  fillColor: string;
  fillPoints: number[];
}

const INITIAL: ModalsState = {
  editing: null,
  floatingPos: null,
  contextMenu: null,
  stampEditorTarget: null,
  diagramEditorTarget: null,
  fillMode: false,
  fillColor: '#fbbf24',
  fillPoints: [],

/**
 * UI-СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РјРѕРґР°Р»РѕРє Рё transient-СЂРµР¶РёРјРѕРІ.
 *
 * В§ARC-STATE-02: РµРґРёРЅС‹Р№ store + С‚РѕС‡РµС‡РЅС‹Рµ СЃРµР»РµРєС‚РѕСЂС‹.
 * В§ARC-STATE-06: slices editing / floating / contextMenu / stamp / diagram / fill
 *               вЂ” РІ РѕРґРЅРѕРј store, РЅРѕ СЃ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹РјРё РјРµС‚РѕРґР°РјРё-РјСѓС‚Р°С‚РѕСЂР°РјРё.
 *
 * Р’СЃРµ РјСѓС‚Р°С†РёРё РёРґСѓС‚ С‡РµСЂРµР· `setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, ... }))`, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹
 * `SimpleStore` РЅРµ С‚РµСЂСЏР» РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ (РµРіРѕ `setState` вЂ” full replacement).
 *
 * Clipboard Рё styleClipboard вЂ” РЅРµ РІ state, Р° РІ РїСЂРёРІР°С‚РЅС‹С… РїРѕР»СЏС…:
 * РёС… РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РЅРµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°РЅРѕ РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°С‚СЊ СЂРµ-СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂ, Р° `getXxx()` РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ
 * С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂРµ ContextMenu / РїСЂРё commit.
 */
export class ModalsStore extends SimpleStore<ModalsState> {
  private clipboard: CanvasElement[] = [];
  private styleClipboard: Partial<CanvasElement> | null = null;

  constructor() {
    super(INITIAL);
  }

  /* ---------- editing ---------- */

  openEditor(state: EditingState) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      editing: state,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
    }));
  }

  setEditing(state: EditingState | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, editing: state }));
  }

  closeEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, editing: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- floating ---------- */

  setFloatingPos(p: { x: number; y: number } | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, floatingPos: p }));
  }

  /* ---------- context menu ---------- */

  setContextMenu(m: ContextMenuState | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, contextMenu: m }));
  }

  /* ---------- stamp editor ---------- */

  openStampEditor(id: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      stampEditorTarget: id,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
      editing: null,
    }));
  }

  closeStampEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, stampEditorTarget: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- diagram editor ---------- */

  openDiagramEditor(id: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      diagramEditorTarget: id,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
      editing: null,
    }));
  }

  closeDiagramEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, diagramEditorTarget: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- fill mode ---------- */

  setFillMode(v: boolean) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      fillMode: v,
      fillPoints: v ? prev.fillPoints : [],
    }));
  }

  setFillColor(c: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, fillColor: c }));
  }

  setFillPoints(pts: number[]) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, fillPoints: pts }));
  }

  /* ---------- clipboard ---------- */

  copy(elements: CanvasElement[]) {
    this.clipboard = elements.map(
      (el) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement,
    );
  }

  getClipboard(): CanvasElement[] {
    return this.clipboard;
  }

  copyStyle(element: CanvasElement) {
    const {
      id: _id,
      x: _x,
      y: _y,
      rotation: _r,
      type: _t,
      ...rest
    } = element as any;
    this.styleClipboard = rest as Partial<CanvasElement>;
  }

  getStyleClipboard(): Partial<CanvasElement> | null {
    return this.styleClipboard;
  }

  clearStyle() {
    this.styleClipboard = null;
  }
}

import { SimpleStore } from '../../shared/store/store';

export interface SelectionState {
  selectedIds: string[];
}

export class SelectionStore extends SimpleStore<SelectionState> {
  constructor() { super({ selectedIds: [] }); }
  select(id: string) { this.setState({ selectedIds: [id] }); }
  toggle(id: string) {
    this.setState((s) => ({
      selectedIds: s.selectedIds.includes(id)
        ? s.selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== id)
        : [...s.selectedIds, id],
    }));
  }
  setIds(ids: string[]) { this.setState({ selectedIds: ids }); }
  clear() { this.setState({ selectedIds: [] }); }
}

FILE: services\snap.ts

import type { Slide, CanvasElement } from '../shared/types';
import { GRID_SIZE, SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '../shared/constants';

export interface SnapResult {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  v: number[];
  h: number[];
}

export function computeSnap(
  element: CanvasElement,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide,
  options: { snapToGrid?: boolean; snapToElements?: boolean; snapToCenter?: boolean; snapToEdges?: boolean } = {}
): SnapResult {
  const { snapToGrid = true, snapToElements = true, snapToCenter = true, snapToEdges = true } = options;

  let minDistX = SNAP_THRESHOLD + 1;
  let bestSnapX = x;
  let guidesX: number[] = [];

  let minDistY = SNAP_THRESHOLD + 1;
  let bestSnapY = y;
  let guidesY: number[] = [];

  // РС‰РµРј РёРґРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃРЅР°Рї РїРѕ X
  const considerX = (targetX: number, guideVal: number) => {
    const dist = Math.abs(x - targetX);
    if (dist <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) {
      if (dist < minDistX) {
        minDistX = dist;
        bestSnapX = targetX;
        guidesX = [guideVal];
      } else if (Math.abs(dist - minDistX) < 1) {
        // Р•СЃР»Рё СЂР°СЃСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РѕРґРёРЅР°РєРѕРІРѕРµ, РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј Р»РёРЅРёСЋ, РЅРѕ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РµС‘ РµС‰Рµ РЅРµС‚
        if (!guidesX.includes(guideVal)) guidesX.push(guideVal);
      }
    }
  };

  // РС‰РµРј РёРґРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃРЅР°Рї РїРѕ Y
  const considerY = (targetY: number, guideVal: number) => {
    const dist = Math.abs(y - targetY);
    if (dist <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) {
      if (dist < minDistY) {
        minDistY = dist;
        bestSnapY = targetY;
        guidesY = [guideVal];
      } else if (Math.abs(dist - minDistY) < 1) {
        if (!guidesY.includes(guideVal)) guidesY.push(guideVal);
      }
    }
  };

  // 1. РЎРЅР°Рї Рє СЃРµС‚РєРµ
  if (snapToGrid) {
    const gridX = Math.round(x / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;
    const gridY = Math.round(y / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;
    considerX(gridX, gridX);
    considerY(gridY, gridY);
  }

  // 2. РЎРЅР°Рї Рє РґСЂСѓРіРёРј СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р°Рј
  if (snapToElements || snapToCenter || snapToEdges) {
    const otherElements = slide.elements.filter(
      (e) => e.id !== element.id && e.type !== 'connector' && e.type !== 'line' && !e.locked
    );

    for (const other of otherElements) {
      if (snapToEdges) {
        // Р›РµРІС‹Рµ Рё РїСЂР°РІС‹Рµ РєСЂР°СЏ
        considerX(other.x, other.x);
        considerX(other.x + other.width - element.width, other.x + other.width);
        
        // Р’РµСЂС…РЅРёРµ Рё РЅРёР¶РЅРёРµ РєСЂР°СЏ
        considerY(other.y, other.y);
        considerY(other.y + other.height - element.height, other.y + other.height);
      }

      if (snapToCenter) {
        // Р¦РµРЅС‚СЂС‹
        const otherCx = other.x + other.width / 2;
        const otherCy = other.y + other.height / 2;
        
        considerX(otherCx - element.width / 2, otherCx);
        considerY(otherCy - element.height / 2, otherCy);
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    x: bestSnapX,
    y: bestSnapY,
    v: guidesX,
    h: guidesY,
  };
}

FILE: shared\store\store.ts

import { useSyncExternalStore, useCallback } from 'react';

export type Listener = () => void;

export class SimpleStore<T> {
  private state: T;
  private listeners = new Set<Listener>();

  constructor(initialState: T) {
    this.state = initialState;
  }

  getState = (): T => this.state;

  setState = (nextState: T | ((prev: T) => T)) => {
    this.state = typeof nextState === 'function' 
      ? (nextState as Function)(this.state) 
      : nextState;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  };

  subscribe = (listener: Listener) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

export function useStore<T, S>(store: SimpleStore<T>, selector: (state: T) => S): S {
  const getSnapshot = useCallback(() => selector(store.getState()), [store, selector]);
  return useSyncExternalStore(store.subscribe, getSnapshot, getSnapshot);
}

/* ============================================================
   shared/types/index.ts

   РЈР±СЂР°РЅРѕ readonly РёР· РІСЃРµС… РїРѕР»РµР№, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ РјСѓС‚РёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ РІ Draft<DocumentState>.
   Immer + Draft СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅРѕ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃ mutable-С‚РёРїР°РјРё. Р•СЃР»Рё РѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ

export const isShapeElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is ShapeElement => el.type === 'shape';
export const isLineElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is LineElement => el.type === 'line';

export const isFooterElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is FooterElement => el.type === 'footer';
export const isPlaceholderElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is PlaceholderElement => el.type === 'placeholder';

/**
 * ID generation utilities
 */

let counter = 0;

/**
 * Generate unique ID
 */
export function generateId(): string {
  const timestamp = Date.now().toString(36);
  const random = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8);
  const count = (counter++).toString(36);
  return `${timestamp}-${random}-${count}`;
}

/**
 * Generate ID with prefix
 */
export function generateIdWithPrefix(prefix: string): string {
  return `${prefix}-${generateId()}`;
}

/**
 * Check if string is valid ID
 */
export function isValidId(id: string): boolean {
  return typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0;
}

/// <reference lib="webworker" />
/* ============================================================
   workers/fillWorker.ts
   РўРѕРЅРєР°СЏ РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєР° РЅР°Рґ rasterizeCompositeFills.
   РќРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ Р±СЂРѕСЃР°РµС‚ вЂ” РЅР° Р»СЋР±РѕРµ РёСЃРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ ok:false,
   РёРЅР°С‡Рµ async onmessage Р·Р°РІРёСЃРЅРµС‚ Рё main thread Р±СѓРґРµС‚ Р¶РґР°С‚СЊ РІРµС‡РЅРѕ.
   ============================================================ */

import { rasterizeCompositeFills } from '../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { FillWorkerRequest, FillWorkerResponse } from './protocol';

const ctx = self as unknown as DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope;

ctx.onmessage = async (e: MessageEvent<FillWorkerRequest>) => {
  const { seq, width, height, shapes, fills } = e.data;

  let response: FillWorkerResponse;

  try {
    const result = await rasterizeCompositeFills({ width, height, shapes, fills });
    response = result.ok
      ? { seq, ok: true, blob: result.value }
      : { seq, ok: false, reason: result.reason };
  } catch (err) {
    response = { seq, ok: false, reason: 'all-fills-rejected' };
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error('[fillWorker] unhandled exception', err);
  }

  ctx.postMessage(response);
};

/* ============================================================
   workers/protocol.ts
   РўРёРїС‹ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёР№ main thread <-> fill worker.
   ============================================================ */

import type { FillSpec, FillError } from '../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../shared/types';

export interface FillWorkerRequest {
  seq: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
}

export type FillWorkerResponse =
  | { seq: number; ok: true; blob: Blob }
  | { seq: number; ok: false; reason: FillError };
